- ABBR: $\text{Perimeter of polygon} = \text{Sum of all sides}$

<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Perimeter of the T shape}  &= 2 \times 3 + 5 \times 2 + 8 \times 2 + 12 \\\\
                                  &= 6 + 10 + 16 + 12 \\\\
                                  &= 44 \ \text{cm}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Work out the perimeter and area of the following shape:

![papers/city-of-london-freemens-school/sample/sample-paper-1/s02-q06-i00.png](/assets/papers/city-of-london-freemens-school/sample/sample-paper-1/s02-q06-i00.png "papers/city-of-london-freemens-school/sample/sample-paper-1/s02-q06-i00.png")

-  $P = 9 \ \text{cm}, \ A = 14 \ \text{cm}^2$
-  $P = 14 \ \text{cm}, \ A = 18 \ \text{cm}^2$
-  $P = 18 \ \text{cm}, \ A = 14 \ \text{cm}^2$
-  $P = 18 \ \text{cm}^2, \ A = 14 \ \text{cm}$

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Work out the perimeter and area of the following shape:
<emptyline>
%image%
<emptyline>
-  $P = 9 \ \text{cm}, \ A = 14 \ \text{cm}^2$
-  $P = 14 \ \text{cm}, \ A = 18 \ \text{cm}^2$
-  $P = 18 \ \text{cm}, \ A = 14 \ \text{cm}^2$
-  $P = 18 \ \text{cm}^2, \ A = 14 \ \text{cm}$
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Perimeter} = 2 \times (\text{length} + \text{width})$

- ABBR: $\text{Area of rectangle} = \text{length} \times \text{width}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

As per the diagram,

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Perimeter}   &= 2 \times (7 + 2) \\\\
                    &= 2 \times 9 \\\\
                    &= 18 \ \text{cm} \\\\
\text {Area}        &= 7 \times 2 \\\\
                    &= 14 \ \text{cm} ^ 2
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="option" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Option**

<p>C</p>
</div>
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

Perimeter $=$ $18 \ \text{cm}$

</div>
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

Area $=$ $14 \ \text{cm} ^ 2$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

The diagram below shows a shaded parallelogram drawn inside a rectangle.

![papers/city-of-london-freemens-school/sample/sample-paper-1/s03-q01-i00.png](/assets/papers/city-of-london-freemens-school/sample/sample-paper-1/s03-q01-i00.png "papers/city-of-london-freemens-school/sample/sample-paper-1/s03-q01-i00.png")

What is the area of the shaded parallelogram?

-  $21 \ \text{cm}^2$ 
-  $28 \ \text{cm}^2$ 
-  $32 \ \text{cm}^2$ 
-  $36 \ \text{cm}^2$ 

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">The diagram below shows a shaded parallelogram drawn inside a rectangle.
<emptyline>
%image%
<emptyline>
What is the area of the shaded parallelogram?
<emptyline>
-  $21 \ \text{cm}^2$ 
-  $28 \ \text{cm}^2$ 
-  $32 \ \text{cm}^2$ 
-  $36 \ \text{cm}^2$ 
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Area of parallelogram} = \text{base} \times \text{height}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

As per the diagram,

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Height}                 &= 4 \\\\
\text {Base}                   &= 9 - 2 \\\\
                               &= 7 \\\\
\text {Area}                    &= 7 \times 4 \\\\
                               &= 28 \ \text{cm}^ 2
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="option" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Option**

<p>B</p>
</div>
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$28 \ \text{cm}^ 2$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Work out the perimeter and area of the following shape:

![papers/city-of-london-freemens-school/sample/sample-paper-2/s02-q06-i00.png](/assets/papers/city-of-london-freemens-school/sample/sample-paper-2/s02-q06-i00.png "papers/city-of-london-freemens-school/sample/sample-paper-2/s02-q06-i00.png")

$A$ : P = $9 \ \text{cm}$, A = $14 \ \text{cm}^2 \\\\$
$B$ : P = $14 \ \text{cm}$, A = $18 \ \text{cm}^2 \\\\$
$C$ : P = $18 \ \text{cm}$, A = $14 \ \text{cm}^2 \\\\$
$D$ : P = $18 \ \text{cm}$, A = $14 \ \text{cm}^2 \\\\$

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Work out the perimeter and area of the following shape:
<emptyline>
%image%
<emptyline>
$A$ : P = $9 \ \text{cm}$, A = $14 \ \text{cm}^2 \\\\$
$B$ : P = $14 \ \text{cm}$, A = $18 \ \text{cm}^2 \\\\$
$C$ : P = $18 \ \text{cm}$, A = $14 \ \text{cm}^2 \\\\$
$D$ : P = $18 \ \text{cm}$, A = $14 \ \text{cm}^2 \\\\$
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Perimeter of rectangle} = 2 \times (\text{length} + \text{width})$

- ABBR: $\text{Area of rectangle} = \text{length} \times \text{width}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

As per the diagram,

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Perimeter}                                                   &= 2 \times (7 + 2) \\\\
                                                                    &= 2 \times 9 \\\\
                                                                    &= 18 \ \text{cm} \\\\
\text {Area}                                                        &= 7 \times 2 \\\\
                                                                    &= 14 \ \text{cm} ^ 2
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="option" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

</div>
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$P = 18 \ \text{cm}$

</div>
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$A = 14 \ \text{cm} ^ 2$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

The diagram below shows a shaded parallelogram drawn inside a rectangle.

![papers/city-of-london-freemens-school/sample/sample-paper-2/s03-q01-i00.png](/assets/papers/city-of-london-freemens-school/sample/sample-paper-2/s03-q01-i00.png "papers/city-of-london-freemens-school/sample/sample-paper-2/s03-q01-i00.png")

What is the area of the shaded parallelogram?

$A$ : $21 \ \text{cm}^2 \\\\$
$B$ : $28 \ \text{cm}^2 \\\\$
$C$ : $32 \ \text{cm}^2 \\\\$
$D$ : $36 \ \text{cm}^2 \\\\$

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">The diagram below shows a shaded parallelogram drawn inside a rectangle.
<emptyline>
%image%
<emptyline>
What is the area of the shaded parallelogram?
<emptyline>
$A$ : $21 \ \text{cm}^2 \\\\$
$B$ : $28 \ \text{cm}^2 \\\\$
$C$ : $32 \ \text{cm}^2 \\\\$
$D$ : $36 \ \text{cm}^2 \\\\$
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Area of parallelogram} = \text{base} \times \text{height}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

As per the diagram,

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Height}                 &= 4 \\\\
\text {Base}                   &= 9 - 2 \\\\
                               &= 7 \\\\
\text {Area}                    &= 7 \times 4 \\\\
                               &= 28 \ \text{cm}^ 2
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="option" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Option**

<p>B</p>
</div>
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$28 \ \text{cm}^ 2$

</div>
</div>

</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

A rectangle is $6 \ \text{cm}$ longer than it is wide. Its perimeter is $32 \ \text{cm}$. Find its area.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">A rectangle is $6 \ \text{cm}$ longer than it is wide. Its perimeter is $32 \ \text{cm}$. Find its area.
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Perimeter of rectangle} = 2 \times (\text{length} + \text{width})$

- ABBR: $\text{Area of rectangle} = \text{length} \times \text{width}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let the width (in cm) $= a$

Length $= 6 + a$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
2 \times (a + (6 + a))         &= 32 \\\\
2 \times (2a + 6)              &= 32 \\\\
4a + 12                        &= 32 \\\\
4a                             &= 32 - 12 \\\\
a                              &= \dfrac {20} {4} \\\\
a                              &= \dfrac {5 \times 4} {4} \\\\
a                              &= \dfrac {5 \times \cancel {4} } {\cancel {4} } \\\\
a                              &= 5 \ \text{cm}
\end{aligned}
$

Let's find the area of the rectangle.

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Area}  &= 5 \times (6 + 5) \\\\
             &= 5 \times 11 \\\\
             &= 55 \ \text{cm}^2
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$55 \ \text{ cm}^2$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Tips Raw</strong>
</div>

</code></pre>

<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Formulas Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">- rtq_abbr_formula_katex_expanded_perimeter_rectangle
- rtq_abbr_formula_katex_area_rectangle_expanded
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>

<emptyline>

<emptyline>
As per the question,
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
2 \times (a + (6 + a))         &= 32 \\\\
2 \times (2a + 6)              &= 32 \\\\
4a + 12                        &= 32 \\\\
4a                             &= 32 - 12 \\\\
a                              &= \dfrac {20} {4} \\\\
a                              &= \dfrac {5 \times 4} {4} \\\\
a                              &= \dfrac {5 \times \cancel {4} } {\cancel {4} } \\\\
a                              &= 5 \ \text{cm}
\end{aligned}
$
<emptyline>
Let's find the area of the rectangle.
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Area}  &= 5 \times (6 + 5) \\\\
             &= 5 \times 11 \\\\
             &= 55 \ \text{cm}^2
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

![papers/colfes-school/sample/sample-paper/s01-q15-i00.png](/assets/papers/colfes-school/sample/sample-paper/s01-q15-i00.png "papers/colfes-school/sample/sample-paper/s01-q15-i00.png")

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

![papers/dame-alice-owens-school/sample/sample-paper-1/s01-q34-i00.png](/assets/papers/dame-alice-owens-school/sample/sample-paper-1/s01-q34-i00.png "papers/dame-alice-owens-school/sample/sample-paper-1/s01-q34-i00.png")

If each side is $6 \ \text{centimetres}$ long, calculate the perimeter.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%image%
<emptyline>
If each side is $6 \ \text{centimetres}$ long, calculate the perimeter.
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Perimeter of regular polygon} = \text{n} \times \text{side}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

As per the diagram,

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Perimeter}   &= 8 \times \text {side} \\\\
                    &= 8 \times 6 \\\\
                    &= 48 \ \text{cm}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

John's garden is a rectangle measuring $8 \ \text{m}$ by $5 \ \text{m}$. He wants to pave the garden using slabs which are $1 \ \text{m}$ by $0.5 \ \text{m}$.

How many slabs will he need?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">John's garden is a rectangle measuring $8 \ \text{m}$ by $5 \ \text{m}$. He wants to pave the garden using slabs which are $1 \ \text{m}$ by $0.5 \ \text{m}$.
<emptyline>
How many slabs will he need?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Area of rectangle} = \text{length} \times \text{width}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Number of slabs}  &= \dfrac {\text{Area of the garden}} {\text{Area of a slab}} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {8 \times 5} {1 \times 0.5} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {10 \times 8 \times 5} {10 \times 0.5} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {10 \times 8 \times 5} {5} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {10 \times 8 \times \cancel {5}} {\cancel {5}} \\\\
                        &= 8 \times 10 \\\\
                        &= 80
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$80$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Tips Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="formulacodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="formulacodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Formulas Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">- rtq_abbr_formula_katex_area_rectangle_expanded
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Number of slabs}  &= \dfrac {\text{Area of the garden}} {\text{Area of a slab}} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {8 \times 5} {1 \times 0.5} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {10 \times 8 \times 5} {10 \times 0.5} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {10 \times 8 \times 5} {5} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {10 \times 8 \times \cancel {5}} {\cancel {5}} \\\\
                        &= 8 \times 10 \\\\
                        &= 80
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="answercodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Option Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="answercodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Key Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="answercodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Answer Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">$80$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

![papers/devonport-high-school-for-boys/2020/sample-paper-5/s01-q43-i00.png](/assets/papers/devonport-high-school-for-boys/2020/sample-paper-5/s01-q43-i00.png "papers/devonport-high-school-for-boys/2020/sample-paper-5/s01-q43-i00.png")

**Question**

![papers/dulwich-college/sample/specimen-paper-c/s01-q15-i00.png](/assets/papers/dulwich-college/sample/specimen-paper-c/s01-q15-i00.png "papers/dulwich-college/sample/specimen-paper-c/s01-q15-i00.png")

<div class="question_envelope rag_not_found subquestion">
<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Calculate the width of one of the tiles.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Calculate the width of one of the tiles.
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Area of square} = \text{side} \times \text{side}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let the width of the tile (in $\text{cm}$) $= w$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
w \times w        &= 400 \\\\
w^2               &= 400 \\\\
w                 &= \sqrt {400} \\\\
w                 &= 20 \ \text{cm}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$20 \ \text{cm}$

</div>
</div>

<li class="questionContainer">
<div class="question_envelope rag_not_found subquestion">
<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

The area of the four tiles combined is $12$ times as large as the area of the hole in the middle.

Calculate the length of one of the tiles.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">The area of the four tiles combined is $12$ times as large as the area of the hole in the middle.
<emptyline>
Calculate the length of one of the tiles.
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Area of rectangle} = \text{length} \times \text{width}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let the length of the tile (in $\text{cm}$) $= l$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Area of four rectangular tiles}      &= 12 \times 400 \ \text{cm} ^2 \\\\
\text {Area of one rectangular tile}        &= \dfrac {12 \times 400} {4} \\\\
                                            &= \dfrac {12 \times 100 \times 4} {4} \\\\
                                            &= \dfrac {12 \times 100 \times \cancel {4}} {\cancel {4}} \\\\
                                            &= 12 \times 100 \\\\
                                            &= 1200 \ \text{cm} ^2
\end{aligned}
$

Let's use this area to find $l$.

$
\begin{aligned}
l \times w      &= 1200 \\\\
l \times 20     &= 1200 \\\\
l               &= \dfrac {1200} {20} \\\\
l               &= \dfrac {60 \times 20} {20} \\\\
l               &= \dfrac {60 \times \cancel {20}} {\cancel {20}} \\\\
l               &= 60 \ \text{cm}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$60 \ \text{cm}$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Tips Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="formulacodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="formulacodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Formulas Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">- rtq_abbr_formula_katex_area_rectangle_expanded
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Let the length of the tile (in $\text{cm}$) $= l$
<emptyline>

<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Area of four rectangular tiles}      &= 12 \times 400 \ \text{cm} ^2 \\\\
\text {Area of one rectangular tile}        &= \dfrac {12 \times 400} {4} \\\\
                                            &= \dfrac {12 \times 100 \times 4} {4} \\\\
                                            &= \dfrac {12 \times 100 \times \cancel {4}} {\cancel {4}} \\\\
                                            &= 12 \times 100 \\\\
                                            &= 1200 \ \text{cm} ^2
\end{aligned}
$
<emptyline>
Let's use this area to find $l$.
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
l \times w      &= 1200 \\\\
l \times 20     &= 1200 \\\\
l               &= \dfrac {1200} {20} \\\\
l               &= \dfrac {60 \times 20} {20} \\\\
l               &= \dfrac {60 \times \cancel {20}} {\cancel {20}} \\\\
l               &= 60 \ \text{cm}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="answercodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Option Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="answercodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Key Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="answercodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Answer Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">$60 \ \text{cm}$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>
</li>
<li class="questionContainer">
<div class="question_envelope rag_not_found subquestion">
<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Work out the perimeter of one of the tiles, giving your answer in metres. 

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Work out the perimeter of one of the tiles, giving your answer in metres. 
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Perimeter} = 2 \times (\text{length} + \text{width})$
_expanded
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let the perimeter of the tile (in $\text{cm}$) $= p$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
p   &= 2 \times (60 + 20) \\\\
p   &= 2 \times 80 \\\\
p   &= 160 \ \text{cm} \\\\
p   &= 160 \times \dfrac {1} {100} \text{m} \\\\
p   &= \dfrac {160} {100} \\\\
p   &= 1.6 \ \text{m}
\end{aligned}
$

Perimeter of one tile (in metres) $= 1.6$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$1.6 \ \text{m}$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Work out the area and perimeter of this shape. 

Note: all angles are right angles.

![papers/dulwich-college/sample/specimen-paper-d/s01-q17-i00.png](/assets/papers/dulwich-college/sample/specimen-paper-d/s01-q17-i00.png "papers/dulwich-college/sample/specimen-paper-d/s01-q17-i00.png")

<div class="workings">

As per the diagram, the shape can be divided into two rectangles

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Total Area} &= \text {Rectangle 1} + \text {Rectangle 2} \\\\
                   &= (5 \times 14) + (4 \times 8) \\\\
                   &= 70 + 32 \\\\
                   &= 102 \ \text{cm}^2
\end{aligned}
$

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Total Perimeter} &= \text {Sum of all the sides} \\\\
                        &= 14 + 5 + 6 + 4 + 8 + 9 \\\\
                        &= 46 \ \text{cm}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

The area of the rectangle is $55 \ \text{cm}^2$ and both $w$ and $h$ are prime numbers.

Calculate the perimeter of the rectangle.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">The area of the rectangle is $55 \ \text{cm}^2$ and both $w$ and $h$ are prime numbers.
<emptyline>
Calculate the perimeter of the rectangle.
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Perimeter} = 2 \times (\text{length} + \text{width})$
_expanded
- ABBR: $\text{Area of rectangle} = \text{length} \times \text{width}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

As per the question,

Factors of $55 = 1, 5, 11, 55$

$5$ and $11$ are prime numbers.

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Perimeter}   &= 2 \times (5 + 11) \\\\
                    &= 2 \times 16 \\\\
                    &= 32 \ \text{cm}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

Note: all angles are right angles but the diagram has not been drawn to scale.

As per the diagram,

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Total Area}  &= \text {Rectangle 1 + Rectangle 2 + Rectangle 3} \\\\
                    &= (3 \times 16) + (7 \times 10) + (2 \times 20) \\\\
                    &= 48 + 70 + 40 \\\\
                    &= 158 \ \text{cm}^2
\end{aligned}
$

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Perimeter} &= \text {Sum of all the sides} \\\\
                  &= 16 + 3 + 6 + 7 + 10 + 2 + 20 + 12 \\\\
                  &= 76 \ \text{cm}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>

</div>
<div class="papername">
<p>dulwich-college--11-plus--maths--9999--specimen-paper-h:1:21</p>
</div>
<div class="rag">
<p>Waffles Woof</p>
</div>
<div class="rag">

<div class="rag">
<p>rag_wf_prns</p>

</li>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

A piece of wire $30 \ \text{cm}$ long is bent into the shape of a rectangle. 
If the rectangle is twice as long as it is wide, what is its area?  

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">A piece of wire $30 \ \text{cm}$ long is bent into the shape of a rectangle. 
If the rectangle is twice as long as it is wide, what is its area?  
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Perimeter of rectangle} = 2 \times (\text{length} + \text{width})$

- ABBR: $\text{Area of rectangle} = \text{length} \times \text{width}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let the width of the rectangle $= a$

Length of the rectangle $= 2a$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
2 \times (2a + a)                         &= 30 \\\\
2 \times 3a                               &= 30 \\\\
6a                                        &= 30 \\\\
a                                         &= \dfrac {30} {6} \\\\
a                                         &= \dfrac {5 \times 6} {6} \\\\
a                                         &= \dfrac {5 \times \cancel {6}} {\cancel {6}} \\\\
a                                         &= 5 \ \text{cm}
\end{aligned}
$

Width of the rectangle $= 5 \ \text{cm}$

Length of the rectangle $= 10 \ \text{cm}$

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Area of the rectangle}        &= 5 \times 10 \\\\
                                    &= 50 \ \text{cm} ^2
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$50 \ \text{cm} ^2$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">

<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Let the width of the rectangle $= a$
<emptyline>
Length of the rectangle $= 2a$
<emptyline>
As per the question,
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
2 \times (2a + a)                         &= 30 \\\\
2 \times 3a                               &= 30 \\\\
6a                                        &= 30 \\\\
a                                         &= \dfrac {30} {6} \\\\
a                                         &= \dfrac {5 \times 6} {6} \\\\
a                                         &= \dfrac {5 \times \cancel {6}} {\cancel {6}} \\\\
a                                         &= 5 \ \text{cm}
\end{aligned}
$
<emptyline>
Width of the rectangle $= 5 \ \text{cm}$
<emptyline>
Length of the rectangle $= 10 \ \text{cm}$
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Area of the rectangle}        &= 5 \times 10 \\\\
                                    &= 50 \ \text{cm} ^2
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

A room measures $8 \ \text{m}$ by $20 \ \text{m}$.
Isabelle wants to tile the floor with square tiles.

What is the length of the largest tile she can use so that all of the floor will be covered?

-  $2 \ \text{m}$
-  $3 \ \text{m}$
-  $4 \ \text{m}$
-  $5 \ \text{m}$

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">A room measures $8 \ \text{m}$ by $20 \ \text{m}$.
Isabelle wants to tile the floor with square tiles.
<emptyline>
What is the length of the largest tile she can use so that all of the floor will be covered?
<emptyline>
-  $2 \ \text{m}$
-  $3 \ \text{m}$
-  $4 \ \text{m}$
-  $5 \ \text{m}$
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

As per the question, 

Length $= 8 \ \text{m}$

Width  $= 20 \ \text{m}$

To use complete tiles, without cutting, the size of the square tile must divide both the length and width of the room. 

Let's find the highest common factor of $8$ and $20$.

Factors of $8 = 1, 2, \bold 4, 8$

Factors of $20 = 1, 2, \bold 4, 5, 20$

The highest common factor of $8$ and $20=4$.

So, the length of the largest square Isabelle can use so that all the floor will be covered $= 4 \ \text{m}$
</div>
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Alternative solution

As per the question,

LCM of 8 and 20 $= 40$

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Area of room}                                   &= \text{Length} \times \text{Width} \\\\
                                                      &= 8 \ \text{m} \times 20 \ \text{m}\\\\
                                                      &= 160 \ \text{m}^2 \\\\
\text{Largest square tile that can be used}           &= \dfrac {160} {40} \\\\
                                                      &= 4 \ \text{m}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="option" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Option**

<p>C</p>
</div>
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$4 \ \text{m}$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

This shape is made up of $3$ identical rectangles.

![papers/eltham-college/2019/sample-paper-1/s01-q17-i00.png](/assets/papers/eltham-college/2019/sample-paper-1/s01-q17-i00.png "papers/eltham-college/2019/sample-paper-1/s01-q17-i00.png")

What is the perimeter of the shape?

-  $50 \ \text{m}$
-  $66 \ \text{m}$
-  $72 \ \text{m}$
-  $54 \ \text{m}$

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">This shape is made up of $3$ identical rectangles.
<emptyline>
%image%
<emptyline>
What is the perimeter of the shape?
<emptyline>
-  $50 \ \text{m}$
-  $66 \ \text{m}$
-  $72 \ \text{m}$
-  $54 \ \text{m}$
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Perimeter of rectangle} = 2 \times (\text{length} + \text{width})$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

*ABBR: TODO:: Add drawing starts.*

{/* ![missing image](/papers/missing_image.svg) */}
TODOIMAGE


*TODO:: Show the given diagram divided into three rectangles with measurements shown.*

*ABBR: TODO:: Add drawing ends.*


As per the diagram, the shape can be divided into three rectangles

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Perimeter of the shape}                  &= \text{Perimeter of 3 rectangles} - \text{4 Overlapping width} \\\\
                                               &= 3 \times (2 \times (8 + 3)) - 4 \times 3 \\\\
                                               &= 3 \times (2 \times 11) - 12 \\\\
                                               &= 3 \times 22 - 12 \\\\
                                               &= 66 - 12 \\\\
                                               &= 54 \ \text{m}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="option" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Option**

<p>D</p>
</div>
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$54 \ \text{m}$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">

<emptyline>
As per the diagram, the shape can be divided into three rectangles
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Perimeter of the shape}                  &= \text{Perimeter of 3 rectangles} - \text{4 Overlapping width} \\\\
                                               &= 3 \times (2 \times (8 + 3)) - 4 \times 3 \\\\
                                               &= 3 \times (2 \times 11) - 12 \\\\
                                               &= 3 \times 22 - 12 \\\\
                                               &= 66 - 12 \\\\
                                               &= 54 \ \text{m}
\end{aligned}
$

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

When $x = 1.5$ what is the value of the perimeter of the triangular area?

![papers/eltham-college/2020/sample-paper-2/s01-q17-i00.png](/assets/papers/eltham-college/2020/sample-paper-2/s01-q17-i00.png "papers/eltham-college/2020/sample-paper-2/s01-q17-i00.png")

- $12$
- $12.5$
- $13$
- $13.5$

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">When $x = 1.5$ what is the value of the perimeter of the triangular area?
<emptyline>
%image%
<emptyline>
- $12$
- $12.5$
- $13$
- $13.5$
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Perimeter of polygon} = \text{Sum of all sides}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

As per the diagram,

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Perimeter}                               &= 3(x +1) + 2(x - 2) + 4x \\\\
                                               &= 3(1.5 +1) + 2(1.5 - 2) + 4 \times 1.5 \\\\
                                               &= 3 \times 2.5 + 2 \times (-0.5) + 6 \\\\
                                               &= 7.5 - 1 + 6 \\\\
                                               &= 6.5 + 6 \\\\
                                               &= 12.5
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="option" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Option**

<p>B</p>
</div>
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$12.5$

</div>
</div>

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">

</code></pre>

<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Area of rectangle} = \text{length} \times \text{width}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

As per the diagram,

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Area}                        &= 5 \times 2 \\\\
                                   &= 10 \ \text{cm}^{2}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$10 \text{cm}^{2}$

</div>
</div>

**Question**

How many triangles of the size shown will fit into the rectangle above?

Neither diagram is drawn to scale.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">

<div class="workings">

<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

$

                                                    &= \dfrac {10} {\dfrac {1} {2}} \\\\
                                                    &= 10 \times 2 \\\\
                                                    &= 20
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

$20$

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Safiya draws a rectangle.

She then adds $10 \ \text{cm}$ to each side.

What has the perimeter increased by? 

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Safiya draws a rectangle.
<emptyline>
She then adds $10 \ \text{cm}$ to each side.
<emptyline>
What has the perimeter increased by? 
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Perimeter of rectangle} = 2 \times (\text{length} + \text{width})$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let the original length $= l$.

Let the original width $= w$.

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Perimeter of original rectangle}              &= 2 \times (l + w)
\end{aligned}
$

As per the question,

Increased length $= l + 10$

Increased width $= w + 10$

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Perimeter of increased rectangle}    &= 2 \times (l + 10 + w + 10) \\\\
                                            &= 2 \times (l + w + 20) \\\\
                                            &= 2 \times (l + w) + 40 \\\\
                                            &= \text {Perimeter of original rectangle} + 40
\end{aligned}
$

The perimeter is increased by $40$.
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$40 \ \text{cm}$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">- rtq_abbr_formula_katex_expanded_perimeter_rectangle
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>

<emptyline>
Let the original width $= w$.
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}

\end{aligned}
$
<emptyline>

<emptyline>

<emptyline>
Increased width $= w + 10$
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Perimeter of increased rectangle}    &= 2 \times (l + 10 + w + 10) \\\\
                                            &= 2 \times (l + w + 20) \\\\
                                            &= 2 \times (l + w) + 40 \\\\
                                            &= \text {Perimeter of original rectangle} + 40
\end{aligned}
$
<emptyline>
The perimeter is increased by $40$.
</code></pre>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

A rectangle has an area of $20  \ \text{cm}^{2}$. Its sides are doubled to make a new rectangle.
What is the area of new rectangle?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">A rectangle has an area of $20  \ \text{cm}^{2}$. Its sides are doubled to make a new rectangle.
What is the area of new rectangle?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Area of rectangle} = \text{length} \times \text{width}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let the length of the rectangle $= l$

Let the width of the rectangle $= w$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Area of rectanlge}   &= 20 \\\\
l \times w                  &= 20 \ \text{cm}^2
\end{aligned}
$

After sides are doubled,

New length $= 2 \times l$

New width $= 2 \times w$

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {New area}    &= (2 \times l) \times (2 \times w) \\\\
                    &= 4 \times (l \times w) \\\\
                    &= 4 \times 20 \ \text{cm} ^2 \\\\
                    &= 80 \ \text{cm} ^2
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$80 \ \text{cm} ^2$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Tips Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>

<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Let the length of the rectangle $= l$
<emptyline>
Let the width of the rectangle $= w$
<emptyline>
As per the question,
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Area of rectanlge}   &= 20 \\\\
l \times w                  &= 20 \ \text{cm}^2
\end{aligned}
$
<emptyline>
After sides are doubled,
<emptyline>
New length $= 2 \times l$
<emptyline>
New width $= 2 \times w$
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
\text {New area}    &= (2 \times l) \times (2 \times w) \\\\
                    &= 4 \times (l \times w) \\\\
                    &= 4 \times 20 \ \text{cm} ^2 \\\\
                    &= 80 \ \text{cm} ^2
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

The formula for the area of a circle is $A = \pi r^2$. For the following task use $\pi = 3.14$.
A circle of radius $10 \ \text{cm}$ is inscribed inside a square as shown. Find the area of the shaded region. 

![papers/hampton-court-house/sample/sample-paper-2/s01-q19-i00.png](/assets/papers/hampton-court-house/sample/sample-paper-2/s01-q19-i00.png "papers/hampton-court-house/sample/sample-paper-2/s01-q19-i00.png")

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">The formula for the area of a circle is $A = \pi r^2$. For the following task use $\pi = 3.14$.
A circle of radius $10 \ \text{cm}$ is inscribed inside a square as shown. Find the area of the shaded region. 
<emptyline>
%image%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Area of square} = \text{side} \times \text{side}$

- ABBR: $\text{Area of circle} = \text{3.14} \times \text{radius} \times \text{radius}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

As per the diagram,

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Area of square}                   &= 20 \times 20 \\\\
                                        &= 400 \ \text{cm}^2 \\\\
\text{Area of circle}                   &= 3.14 \times 10 \times 10 \\\\
                                        &= 314 \ \text{cm}^2 \\\\
\text{Area of shaded region}            &= \text{area of square - area of circle} \\\\
                                        &= 400 - 314 \\\\
                                        &= 86 \ \text{cm}^2
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$86 \text{cm}^2$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Tanya cuts out a piece of card into a rectangle. The rectangle is twice as long as it is wide. 
The area of the rectangle is $128 \ \text{cm}^2$.

What is the perimeter of rectangle?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Tanya cuts out a piece of card into a rectangle. The rectangle is twice as long as it is wide. 
The area of the rectangle is $128 \ \text{cm}^2$.
<emptyline>
What is the perimeter of rectangle?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Area of rectangle} = \text{length} \times \text{width}$

- ABBR: $\text{Perimeter of rectangle} = 2 \times (\text{length} + \text{width})$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let the width of the rectangle $= a$

Length $= 2a$

As per the question, 

$
\begin{aligned}
2a \times a                     &= 128 \\\\
2a ^ 2                          &= 128 \\\\
a ^ 2                           &= \dfrac {128} {2} \\\\
a ^ 2                           &= \dfrac {64 \times 2} {2} \\\\
a ^ 2                           &= \dfrac {64 \times \cancel {2}} {\cancel {2}} \\\\
a ^ 2                           &= 64 \\\\
a                               &= \sqrt {64} \\\\
a                               &= 8 \ \text{cm}
\end{aligned}
$

Width of the rectangle $= 8 \ \text{cm}$

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Length of the rectangle}  &= 2 \times 8 \\\\
                                &= 16 \ \text{cm}
\end{aligned}
$

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Perimeter}               &= 2 \times (16 + 8) \\\\
                                &= 2 \times 24 \\\\
                                &= 48 \ \text{cm}
\end{aligned}
$ 
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$48 \ \text{cm}$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Tips Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="formulacodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="formulacodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Formulas Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">- rtq_abbr_formula_katex_area_rectangle_expanded
- rtq_abbr_formula_katex_expanded_perimeter_rectangle
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>

</div>
</div>

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

What is the area covered by Mr Green's lawn?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">What is the area covered by Mr Green's lawn?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

</div>

<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

- ABBR: $\text{Area of rectangle} = \text{length} \times \text{width}$

<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

{/* ![missing image](/papers/missing_image.svg) */}
TODOIMAGE


*TODO:: Area of flower bed can be divided into left and top portion.*

*ABBR: TODO:: Add drawing ends.*


As per the diagram,

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Area of lawn} &= \text {Area of garden} - \text {Area of flower bed} - \text {Area of patio} \\\\
                     &= 23 \times 9 - (23 \times 2 + (9 - 2) \times 2) - 3 \times 8 \\\\
                     &= 207 - 46 - 7 \times 2 - 24 \\\\
                     &= 161 - 14 - 24 \\\\
                     &= 123 \ \text{m}^2
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$123 \ \text{m} ^ 2$

</div>

<li class="questionContainer">
<div class="question_envelope rag_not_found subquestion">
<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Mr Green buys $6$ flower pots. Flower pots cost $\pounds 4.00$ each.

Mr Green has a voucher for $15\%$ off.

How much does Mr Green spend on flower pots if he uses the voucher?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Mr Green buys $6$ flower pots. Flower pots cost $\pounds 4.00$ each.
<emptyline>
Mr Green has a voucher for $15\%$ off.
<emptyline>
How much does Mr Green spend on flower pots if he uses the voucher?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

If the price is reduced by $15 \%$, cost for the flower pots will be $100 - 15 = 85 \%$ of the total cost.

Using this we get,

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Mr Green spend}   &= 85 \% \ \text {of} \ \text {Total cost of flower pots} \\\\
                         &= \dfrac {85} {100} \times 4 \times 6 \\\\
                         &= \dfrac {85} {100} \times 24 \\\\
                         &= \dfrac {85 \times 24} {100} \\\\
                         &= \dfrac {2040} {100} \\\\
                         &= \pounds 20.40
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$\pounds 20.40$

</div>
</div>

<li class="questionContainer">
<div class="question_envelope rag_not_found subquestion">
<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Mr Green wants to lay paving stones on his patio.
The stones are rectangles measuring $50 \ \text{cm} \times 20 \ \text{cm}$.

How many paving stones are needed to pave the patio?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Mr Green wants to lay paving stones on his patio.
The stones are rectangles measuring $50 \ \text{cm} \times 20 \ \text{cm}$.
<emptyline>
How many paving stones are needed to pave the patio?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
- ABBR: It is really important to use the right units in calculations and convert where required.
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Area} = \text{length} \times \text{width}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

The patio measurements is given in metres while the paving stones measurement is given in cm.

Let's convert the wall measurements into $\text{cm}$.

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Number of paving stones}  &= \dfrac {\text {Area of the patio} } {\text {Area of a paving stones} } \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {8 \ \text{m} \times 3 \ \text{m} } { 50 \ \text{cm} \times 20 \ \text{cm} } \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {8 \times 100  \ \text{cm} \times 3 \times 100 \ \text{cm} } {50 \ \text{cm} \times 20 \ \text{cm} } \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {8 \times 100 \times 3 \times 100 } { 50  \times 20 } \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {8 \times 50 \times 2 \times 3 \times 20 \times 5 } { 50  \times 20 }  \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {8 \times \cancel {50} \times 2 \times 3 \times \cancel {20} \times 5 } { \cancel{50}  \times \cancel{20} }  \\\\
                                &= 8 \times 2 \times 3 \times 5  \\\\
                                &= 240
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

The number of paving stones needed is $=$ $240$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

</code></pre>

<div class="formulacodeblock_title">

</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">- rtq_abbr_formula_katex_area_rectangle
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">The patio measurements is given in metres while the paving stones measurement is given in cm.
<emptyline>
Let's convert the wall measurements into $\text{cm}$.
<emptyline>
$

                                &= \dfrac {8 \ \text{m} \times 3 \ \text{m} } { 50 \ \text{cm} \times 20 \ \text{cm} } \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {8 \times 100  \ \text{cm} \times 3 \times 100 \ \text{cm} } {50 \ \text{cm} \times 20 \ \text{cm} } \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {8 \times 100 \times 3 \times 100 } { 50  \times 20 } \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {8 \times 50 \times 2 \times 3 \times 20 \times 5 } { 50  \times 20 }  \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {8 \times \cancel {50} \times 2 \times 3 \times \cancel {20} \times 5 } { \cancel{50}  \times \cancel{20} }  \\\\
                                &= 8 \times 2 \times 3 \times 5  \\\\
                                &= 240
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<li class="questionContainer">
<div class="question_envelope rag_not_found subquestion">
<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

The area of the flower bed is $60 \ \text{m}^2$.

Three sacks of fertilizer are needed for every $4 \ \text{m}^2$ of flower bed.

Sacks cost $\pounds 10$ each, but are on offer: *buy four sacks and get a fifth sack free*.

How much does it cost to fertilize the flower bed?              

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">The area of the flower bed is $60 \ \text{m}^2$.
<emptyline>
Three sacks of fertilizer are needed for every $4 \ \text{m}^2$ of flower bed.
<emptyline>
Sacks cost $\pounds 10$ each, but are on offer: *buy four sacks and get a fifth sack free*.
<emptyline>
How much does it cost to fertilize the flower bed?              
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

As per the question,

Sacks needed for $1 \ \text{m} ^ 2 = \dfrac {3} {4}$.

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Total sacks needed}  &= \dfrac {3} {4} \times 60 \\\\
                            &= \dfrac {3 \times 60} {4} \\\\
                            &= \dfrac {3 \times 15 \times 4} {4} \\\\
                            &= \dfrac {3 \times 15 \times \cancel {4} } {\cancel {4}} \\\\
                            &= 3 \times 15 \\\\
                            &= 45
\end{aligned}
$

There is an offer for every four sacks get fifth free so for $45$ sacks number of sacks free $\dfrac {45} {5} = 9$. Using this we get,

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Total cost}      &= (45 - 9) \times 10 \\\\
                        &= 36 \times 10 \\\\
                        &= \pounds 360
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

The total cost is $=$ $\pounds 360$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Tips Raw</strong>
</div>

</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="formulacodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="formulacodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Formulas Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">As per the question,
<emptyline>
Sacks needed for $1 \ \text{m} ^ 2 = \dfrac {3} {4}$.
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Total sacks needed}  &= \dfrac {3} {4} \times 60 \\\\
                            &= \dfrac {3 \times 60} {4} \\\\
                            &= \dfrac {3 \times 15 \times 4} {4} \\\\
                            &= \dfrac {3 \times 15 \times \cancel {4} } {\cancel {4}} \\\\
                            &= 3 \times 15 \\\\
                            &= 45
\end{aligned}
$
<emptyline>
There is an offer for every four sacks get fifth free so for $45$ sacks number of sacks free $\dfrac {45} {5} = 9$. Using this we get,
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Total cost}      &= (45 - 9) \times 10 \\\\
                        &= 36 \times 10 \\\\
                        &= \pounds 360
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Calculate the perimeter of this shape stating your units very carefully.

![papers/james-allens-girls-school/sample/sample-paper-1/s01-q20-i00.png](/assets/papers/james-allens-girls-school/sample/sample-paper-1/s01-q20-i00.png "papers/james-allens-girls-school/sample/sample-paper-1/s01-q20-i00.png")

</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Calculate the perimeter of this shape stating your units very carefully.

</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
- ABBR: It is really important to use the right units in calculations and convert where required.
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Perimeter of polygon} = \text{Sum of all sides}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let's convert all the lenghts into meters.

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Perimeter of the shape}  &= 80 \ \text{cm} + 1.6 \text{m} + (1.8 \text{m} - 80 \ \text{cm}) + 30 \ \text{cm} + 1.8 \text{m} + (1.6 \text{m} + 30 \ \text{cm}) \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {80} {100} \text{m} + 1.6 \text{m} + (1.8 \text{m} - \dfrac {80} {100} \text{m}) + \dfrac {30} {100} \text{m} + 1.8 \text{m} + (1.6 \text{m} + \dfrac {30} {100} \text{m}) \\\\
                                &= 0.80 + 1.6 + (1.8 - 0.80) + 0.30 + 1.8 + (1.6 + 0.30) \\\\
                                &= 2.40 + 1 + 2.1 + 1.90 \\\\
                                &= 7.4 \ \text{m}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$7.4 \text{m}$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

A tile in the shape of a cross is made by drawing a square of length $10 \ \text{cm}$ 
and then removing four squares of length $2 \ \text{cm}$ from each corner. 

What is the perimeter of the cross shape tile?

![papers/john-lyon-school/sample/sample-paper-1/s01-q23-i00.png](/assets/papers/john-lyon-school/sample/sample-paper-1/s01-q23-i00.png "papers/john-lyon-school/sample/sample-paper-1/s01-q23-i00.png")

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">A tile in the shape of a cross is made by drawing a square of length $10 \ \text{cm}$ 
and then removing four squares of length $2 \ \text{cm}$ from each corner. 
<emptyline>
What is the perimeter of the cross shape tile?
<emptyline>
%image%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Perimeter of square} = 4 \times \text{side}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

As per the diagram,

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Perimeter of cross shape tile} &= \text {Perimeter of the big square} - 8 \times \text {Corner squares} \\\\
                                     &= 4 \times 10 - 8 \times 2 \\\\
                                     &= 40 - 16 \\\\
                                     &= 24 \ \text{cm}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$24 \ \text{cm}$

</div>
</div>
