<div class="question_envelope rag_not_found subquestion">
<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Robert puts three tiles together to make the shape below. What is the perimeter of his shape?

![papers/john-lyon-school/sample/sample-paper-1/s01-q23-s01-i00.png](/assets/papers/john-lyon-school/sample/sample-paper-1/s01-q23-s01-i00.png "papers/john-lyon-school/sample/sample-paper-1/s01-q23-s01-i00.png")

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Robert puts three tiles together to make the shape below. What is the perimeter of his shape?
<emptyline>
%image%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

As per the diagram,

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Perimeter} &= 3 \times \text{Perimeter of tile} - 4 \times \text{Common side between tiles} \\\\
                 &= 3 \times 24 - 4 \times (10 - 4) \\\\
                 &= 72 - 4 \times 6 \\\\
                 &= 72 - 24 \\\\
                 &= 48 \ \text{cm}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$48 \ \text{cm}$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question_envelope rag_not_found subquestion">
<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Ravi put ten tiles together in a similar way. What is the perimeter of his shape?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Ravi put ten tiles together in a similar way. What is the perimeter of his shape?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Perimeter of 10 tiles} &= 10 \times \text{Perimeter of tile} - 18 \times \text{Common side between tiles} \\\\
                             &= 10 \times 24 - 18 \times (10 - 4) \\\\
                             &= 240 - 18 \times 6 \\\\
                             &= 240 - 108 \\\\
                             &= 132 \ \text{cm}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$132 \ \text{cm}$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Find the perimeter of this rectangle:

![papers/kent-college/sample/sample-paper-1/s01-q10-i00.png](/assets/papers/kent-college/sample/sample-paper-1/s01-q10-i00.png "papers/kent-college/sample/sample-paper-1/s01-q10-i00.png")

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Find the perimeter of this rectangle:
<emptyline>
%image%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Perimeter of rectangle} = 2 \times (\text{length} + \text{width})$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

As per the diagram,

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Perimeter}                 &= 2 \times (4.65 + 1.8) \\\\
                                  &= 2 \times 6.45 \\\\
                                  &= 12.90 \ \text{m}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$12.90 \text{m}$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

I want to cut out a circle
radius $6 \ \text{cm}$ from a square
piece of card.
What is the area of the
smallest square I can use?

- $144 \ \text{cm}^2$
- $48 \ \text{cm}^2$
- $36 \ \text{cm}^2$
- $9 \ \text{cm}^2$ 
- impossible to say

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">I want to cut out a circle
radius $6 \ \text{cm}$ from a square
piece of card.
What is the area of the
smallest square I can use?
<emptyline>
- $144 \ \text{cm}^2$
- $48 \ \text{cm}^2$
- $36 \ \text{cm}^2$
- $9 \ \text{cm}^2$ 
- impossible to say
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Area of square} = \text{side} \times \text{side}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

As per the question,

As the radius of circle is $6 \ \text{cm}$, the length of a side of the square must be at least $12 \ \text{cm}$.

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Area of a square}  &= 12 \times 12 \\\\
                          &= 144 \ \text{cm} ^ 2
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="option" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Option**

<p>A</p>
</div>
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$144 \ \text{cm} ^ 2$

</div>
</div>

**Question**

What is the area of the shaded diamond below? **[Diagram not to scale]**

![papers/latymer-upper-school/2020/sample-paper-1/s01-q18-i00.png](/assets/papers/latymer-upper-school/2020/sample-paper-1/s01-q18-i00.png "papers/latymer-upper-school/2020/sample-paper-1/s01-q18-i00.png") 

A: $34 \ \text{cm}^2$ 

B: $264 \ \text{cm}^2$ 

C: $198 \ \text{cm}^2$  

D: $132 \ \text{cm}^2$  

E: $66 \ \text{cm}^2$ 

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">What is the area of the shaded diamond below? **[Diagram not to scale]**
<emptyline>
%image% 
<emptyline>
A: $34 \ \text{cm}^2$ 
<emptyline>
B: $264 \ \text{cm}^2$ 
<emptyline>
C: $198 \ \text{cm}^2$  
<emptyline>
D: $132 \ \text{cm}^2$  
<emptyline>
E: $66 \ \text{cm}^2$ 
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

As per the diagram,

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Area of the diamond} &= \dfrac {\text{First diagonal} \times \text{Second diagonal}} {2} \\\\
                           &= \dfrac {12 \times 22} {2} \\\\
                           &= \dfrac {2 \times 6 \times 22} {2} \\\\
                           &= \dfrac {\cancel {2} \times 6 \times 22} {\cancel {2}} \\\\
                           &= 6 \times 22 \\\\
                           &= 132 \ \text{cm} ^ 2
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="option" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Option**

<p>D</p>
</div>
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$132 \ \text{cm} ^ 2$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

What is the area of the shaded triangle below? [Diagram not to scale]

![papers/latymer-upper-school/2020/sample-paper-2/s01-q18-i00.png](/assets/papers/latymer-upper-school/2020/sample-paper-2/s01-q18-i00.png "papers/latymer-upper-school/2020/sample-paper-2/s01-q18-i00.png") 

- $42 \ \text{cm}^2$ 
- $14 \ \text{cm}^2$ 
- $28 \ \text{cm}^2$ 
- $24.5 \ \text{cm}^2$ 
- $17.5 \ \text{cm}^2$

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">

- $14 \ \text{cm}^2$

- $17.5 \ \text{cm}^2$
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">

<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Area of triangle} = \dfrac{1}{2} \times \text{base} \times \text{height}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

As per the diagram, the shape can be divided into two triangles

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Total Area}                  &= \text {Triangle 1} + \text {Triangle 2} \\\\
                                    &= \dfrac {1} {2} \times 3.5 \times 2 + \dfrac {1} {2} \times 3.5 \times 6 \\\\
                                    &= \dfrac {3.5 \times 2} {2} + \dfrac {3.5 \times 2 \times 3} {2} \\\\
                                    &= \dfrac {3.5 \times \cancel {2}} {\cancel {2}} + \dfrac {3.5 \times \cancel {2} \times 3} {\cancel {2}} \\\\
                                    &= 3.5 + 10.5 \\\\
                                    &= 14 \ \text{cm}^2
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>

$

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Find the length of the side of the square.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Find the length of the side of the square.
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Perimeter of square} = 4 \times \text{side}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let the side of the square $= a$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
4 \times a          &= 20 \\\\
4a                  &= 20 \\\\
a                   &= \dfrac {20} {4} \\\\
a                   &= \dfrac {5 \times \cancel {4}} {\cancel {4}} \\\\
a                   &= 5 \ \text{cm}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$5 \ \text{cm}$

</div>
</div>

<li class="questionContainer">
<div class="question_envelope rag_not_found subquestion">
<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

A square has the same area as this rectangle. 

What is the side length of this square?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">A square has the same area as this rectangle. 
<emptyline>
What is the side length of this square?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Area of rectangle} = \text{length} \times \text{width}$

- ABBR: $\text{Area of square} = \text{side} \times \text{side}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let the side of square $= s$

As per the diagram,

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Area of rectangle}      &= 8 \times 2 \\\\
                               &= 16 \ \text{cm}^2
\end{aligned}
$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Area of square} &= \text{Area of rectangle} \\\\
s \times s            &= 16 \\\\
s ^ 2                 &= 16 \\\\
s                     &= \sqrt {16} \\\\
s                     &= 4 \ \text{cm}
\end{aligned}
$

Length of the side of the square is $4 \ \text{cm}$.
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$4 \ \text{cm}$

</div>
</div>

<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>

</div>

</code></pre>
</div>

<li class="questionContainer">
<div class="question_envelope rag_not_found subquestion">
<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

A different square has the same perimeter as this rectangle.

What is the side length of this square?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">A different square has the same perimeter as this rectangle.
<emptyline>
What is the side length of this square?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Perimeter of rectangle} = 2 \times (\text{length} + \text{width})$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let the side of square $= s$

As per the diagram,

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Perimeter of rectangle} &= 2 \times (8 + 2) \\\\
                               &= 2 \times 10 \\\\
                               &= 20 \ \text{cm}
\end{aligned}
$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Perimeter of square} &= \text{Perimeter of rectangle} \\\\
4 \times s                  &= 20 \\\\
s                           &= \dfrac {20} {4} \\\\
s                           &= \dfrac {5 \times 4} {4} \\\\
s                           &= \dfrac {5 \times \cancel {4}} {\cancel {4}} \\\\
s                           &= 5 \ \text{cm}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$5 \ \text{cm}$

</div>
</div>

<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">

</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

s                           &= \dfrac {5 \times \cancel {4}} {\cancel {4}} \\\\
s                           &= 5 \ \text{cm}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">$5 \ \text{cm}$

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Find the perimeter and area of this shape:

![papers/magdalen-college-school/sample/sample-paper-1/s01-q02-i00.png](/assets/papers/magdalen-college-school/sample/sample-paper-1/s01-q02-i00.png "papers/magdalen-college-school/sample/sample-paper-1/s01-q02-i00.png")

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Find the perimeter and area of this shape:
<emptyline>
%image%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Perimeter of polygon} = \text{Sum of all sides}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

As per the diagram,

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Perimeter}   &= 6 + 5 + 9 + 4 \\\\
                    &= 24 \ \text{cm}
\end{aligned}
$

Let the parallel sides of trapezium $= a, b$

Let the height of trapezium $= h$

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Area of trapezium} &= \dfrac {a + b} {2} \times h \\\\
                          &= \dfrac {6 + 9} {2} \times 4 \\\\
                          &= \dfrac {15 \times 4} {2} \\\\
                          &= \dfrac {15 \times 2 \times 2} {2} \\\\
                          &= \dfrac {15 \times 2 \times \cancel {2}} {\cancel {2}} \\\\
                          &= 15 \times 2 \\\\
                          &= 30 \ \text{cm}^2
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$24 \ \text{cm}, \ 30 \ \text{cm}^2$

</div>
</div>

<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

ABBR: $\text{Surface area} &= 2 \times (\text {length} \times \text {width} + \text {length} \times \text {height} + \text {height} \times \text {width})$

As per the diagram,

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Length}              &= 6 \\\\
\text{Width}               &= 10 \\\\
\text{Height}              &= 3 \\\\
\text {Total surface area} &= 2 \times (6 \times 10 + 6 \times 3 + 3 \times 10) \\\\
                           &= 2 \times (60 + 18 + 30) \\\\
                           &= 2 \times 108 \\\\
                           &= 216 \ \text{cm}^2
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

\text{Width}               &= 10 \\\\
\text{Height}              &= 3 \\\\
\text {Total surface area} &= 2 \times (6 \times 10 + 6 \times 3 + 3 \times 10) \\\\
                           &= 2 \times (60 + 18 + 30) \\\\
                           &= 2 \times 108 \\\\
                           &= 216 \ \text{cm}^2
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

A right-angled triangle has the dimensions as shown. What is the area of the unshaded region?

![papers/merchant-taylors-school/sample/practice-paper-3/s01-q13-i00.png](/assets/papers/merchant-taylors-school/sample/practice-paper-3/s01-q13-i00.png "papers/merchant-taylors-school/sample/practice-paper-3/s01-q13-i00.png")

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">A right-angled triangle has the dimensions as shown. What is the area of the unshaded region?
<emptyline>
%image%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Area of triangle} = \dfrac{1}{2} \times \text{base} \times \text{height}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

As per the diagram,

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Area of unshaded region} &= \text {Area of whole triangle} - \text {Area of shaded region} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {1} {2} \times \text {base1} \times \text {height1} - \dfrac {1} {2} \times \text {base2} \times \text {height2} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {1} {2} \times 6 \times 3 - \dfrac {1} {2} \times 4 \times 3 \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {6 \times 3} {2} - \dfrac {4 \times 3} {2} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {3 \times 2 \times 3} {2} - \dfrac {2 \times 2 \times 3} {2} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {3 \times \cancel {2} \times 3} {\cancel {2}} - \dfrac {2 \times \cancel {2} \times 3} {\cancel {2}} \\\\
                                &= 9 - 6 \\\\
                                &= 3 \ \text{cm}^2
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$3 \ \text{cm} ^ 2$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

</div>

</code></pre>
</div>

<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Formulas Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">- rtq_abbr_formula_katex_area_triangle_expanded
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>

<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Area of unshaded region} &= \text {Area of whole triangle} - \text {Area of shaded region} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {1} {2} \times \text {base1} \times \text {height1} - \dfrac {1} {2} \times \text {base2} \times \text {height2} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {1} {2} \times 6 \times 3 - \dfrac {1} {2} \times 4 \times 3 \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {6 \times 3} {2} - \dfrac {4 \times 3} {2} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {3 \times 2 \times 3} {2} - \dfrac {2 \times 2 \times 3} {2} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {3 \times \cancel {2} \times 3} {\cancel {2}} - \dfrac {2 \times \cancel {2} \times 3} {\cancel {2}} \\\\
                                &= 9 - 6 \\\\
                                &= 3 \ \text{cm}^2
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

The diagram shows a rectangle. 

![papers/merchant-taylors-school/sample/practice-paper-3/s01-q17-i00.png](/assets/papers/merchant-taylors-school/sample/practice-paper-3/s01-q17-i00.png "papers/merchant-taylors-school/sample/practice-paper-3/s01-q17-i00.png")

The length of the rectangle is $2n + 1$. 

The width of the rectangle is $3n - 9$. 

Write down an expression for the perimeter of the rectangle. 

Fully simplify your answer.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">The diagram shows a rectangle. 
<emptyline>
%image%
<emptyline>
The length of the rectangle is $2n + 1$. 
<emptyline>
The width of the rectangle is $3n - 9$. 
<emptyline>
Write down an expression for the perimeter of the rectangle. 
<emptyline>
Fully simplify your answer.
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Perimeter of rectangle} = 2 \times (\text{length} + \text{width})$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Perimeter of the rectangle} &= 2 \times ((2 n + 1) + (3 n - 9)) \\\\
                                   &= 2 \times (2 n + 3n + 1 - 9) \\\\
                                   &= 2 \times (5 n - 8) \\\\
                                   &= 10 n - 16
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$10 n - 16$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Tips Raw</strong>
</div>

</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">- rtq_abbr_formula_katex_expanded_perimeter_rectangle
</code></pre>
</div>

<div class="workingcodeblock_title">

$

</div>

</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">$10 n - 16$

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

The perimeter of a rectangle is $22 \ \text{cm}$. One of the sides has length $8 \ \text{cm}$. 
Calculate the lengths of the other three sides.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">The perimeter of a rectangle is $22 \ \text{cm}$. One of the sides has length $8 \ \text{cm}$. 
Calculate the lengths of the other three sides.
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Perimeter of rectangle} = 2 \times (\text{length} + \text{width})$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let the width $= w$ 

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Perimeter of rectangle}      &= 22 \\\\
2 \times (8 + w)                    &= 22 \\\\
16 + 2 \times w                     &= 22 \\\\
2 \times \text w                    &= 22 - 16 \\\\
2 \times \text w                    &= 6 \\\\
w                                   &= \dfrac {6} {2} \\\\
w                                   &= \dfrac {\cancel {2} \times 3} {\cancel {2}} \\\\
w                                   &= 3 \ \text{cm}
\end{aligned}
$

Other three sides of rectangle are 3cm, 3cm, 8cm.
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$\text {3cm, \ 3cm, \ 8cm}$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

</code></pre>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

The area of a square is $0.25 \ \text{m}^2$. What is the perimeter of the square, in $\text{metres}?$ 

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">The area of a square is $0.25 \ \text{m}^2$. What is the perimeter of the square, in $\text{metres}?$ 
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Area of square} = \text{side} \times \text{side}$

- ABBR: $\text{Perimeter of square} = 4 \times \text{side}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let the side of the square $= s$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
s \times s                                              &= 0.25 \\\\
s ^ 2                                                   &= 0.25 \\\\
s                                                       &= \sqrt {0.25} \\\\
s                                                       &= 0.5 \ \text{m} \\\\
\text{Perimeter}                                        &= 4 \times s \\\\
                                                        &= 4 \times 0.25 \\\\
                                                        &= 1 \ \text{m}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$1 \ \text{m}$

</div>
</div>

</code></pre>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

The perimeter of a rectangle is $14 \ \text{cm}$.

If the length of the rectangle is $2 \ \text{cm}$ longer than the width, calculate the area of the rectangle. 

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">The perimeter of a rectangle is $14 \ \text{cm}$.
<emptyline>
If the length of the rectangle is $2 \ \text{cm}$ longer than the width, calculate the area of the rectangle. 
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Area of rectangle} = \text{length} \times \text{width}$

- ABBR: $\text{Perimeter of rectangle} = 2 \times (\text{length} + \text{width})$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let the breadth of rectangle $= x$

Length $= x + 2$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Perimeter}                                       &= 14 \\\\
2 \times((x + 2) + x)                                   &= 14 \\\\
2 \times(2x + 2)                                        &= 14 \\\\
(2x + 2)                                                &= \dfrac {14} {2} \\\\
(2x + 2)                                                &= \dfrac {7 \times 2} {2} \\\\
(2x + 2)                                                &= \dfrac {7 \times \cancel {2}} {\cancel {2}} \\\\
2x + 2                                                  &= 7 \\\\
2x                                                      &= 7 - 2 \\\\
2x                                                      &= 5 \\\\
x                                                       &= \dfrac {5} {2} \\\\
x                                                       &= 2.5 \ \text{cm} \\\\
\text{Length}                                           &= 2.5 + 2 \\\\
                                                        &= 4.5 \ \text{m} \\\\
\text {Area}                                            &= 2.5 \times 4.5 \\\\
                                                        &= 11.25 \ \text{m}^2
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$1.25 \ \text{m}^2$

</div>
</div>

**Question**

What is the area of each individual square?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">What is the area of each individual square?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

</div>

%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned} 
\text{Area of individual square}                    &= \dfrac {\text{Total area}} {\text{No. of squares}} \\\\
                                                    &= \dfrac {20 \text{m}^{2}} {5} \\\\
                                                    &= \dfrac {4 \times 5} {5} \\\\
                                                    &= \dfrac {4 \times \cancel{5}} {\cancel{5}} \\\\
                                                    &= 4 \ \text{m}^{2}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$4 \ \text{m}^{2}$

</div>

**Question**

What is the length of each side of each Individual square?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">

</code></pre>

<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Area of square} = \text{side} \times \text{side}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let each side of the individual square (in m) $= a$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
a \times a                                      &= 4 \\\\
a^{2}                                           &= 4 \\\\
a                                               &= \sqrt {4} \\\\
a                                               &= 2 \ \text{m}
\end{aligned}
$

The length of each side of the individual square $= 2 \ \text{m}$.
</div>

<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">

</code></pre>

<div class="workings">

</div>

<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

                                                    &= 24 \ \text{m}
\end{aligned}
$

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

A square has area $49\ \text{cm}^2$. What is the perimeter of the square?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">A square has area $49\ \text{cm}^2$. What is the perimeter of the square?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Area of square} = \text{side} \times \text{side}$

- ABBR: $\text{Perimeter of square} = 4 \times \text{side}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let side of the square (in cm) $= a$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
a \times a                                      &= 49 \\\\
a^{2}                                           &= 49 \\\\
a                                               &= \sqrt {49} \\\\
a                                               &= 7 \ \text{cm}
\end{aligned}
$

Side of the square $= 7 \ \text{cm}$

Let's find the perimeter of the square.

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Perimeter of the square}                  &= 4 \times 7 \\\\
                                                &= 28 \ \text{cm}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$28 \ \text{cm}$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

</code></pre>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

I have a painting in a frame. The frame is $75 \ \text{cm}$ wide and $65 \ \text{cm}$ high. There is a border $15 \ \text{cm}$ wide around the painting. Find the width and height of the painting.

![papers/reigate-grammar-school/2018/sample-paper-5/s01-q29-i00.png](/assets/papers/reigate-grammar-school/2018/sample-paper-5/s01-q29-i00.png "papers/reigate-grammar-school/2018/sample-paper-5/s01-q29-i00.png")

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">I have a painting in a frame. The frame is $75 \ \text{cm}$ wide and $65 \ \text{cm}$ high. There is a border $15 \ \text{cm}$ wide around the painting. Find the width and height of the painting.
<emptyline>
%image%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

As per the picture,

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Width of the painting}                        &= \text{Total width} - 2 \times \text{Frame height} \\\\
                                                    &= 75 \ \text{cm} - 2 \times 15 \ \text{cm} \\\\
                                                    &= 75 - 30 \\\\
                                                    &= 45 \ \text{cm}
\end{aligned}
$

Let's find the height of the painting.

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Height of the painting}                       &= \text{Total height} - 2 \times \text{Frame height} \\\\
                                                    &= 65 \ \text{cm} - 2 \times 15 \ \text{cm} \\\\
                                                    &= 65 - 30 \\\\
                                                    &= 35 \ \text{cm}
\end{aligned}
$

Width $= 45 \ \text{cm}$

Height $= 35 \ \text{cm}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

Width $= 45 \ \text{cm}$

Height $= 35 \ \text{cm}$

</div>
</div>

                                                    &= 75 \ \text{cm} - 2 \times 15 \ \text{cm} \\\\

                                                    &= 65 \ \text{cm} - 2 \times 15 \ \text{cm} \\\\
                                                    &= 65 - 30 \\\\
                                                    &= 35 \ \text{cm}
\end{aligned}
$
<emptyline>

<emptyline>

</code></pre>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

A square has area $64\ \text{cm}^2$. What is the perimeter of the square? 

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">A square has area $64\ \text{cm}^2$. What is the perimeter of the square? 
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Area of square} = \text{side} \times \text{side}$

- ABBR: $\text{Perimeter of square} = 4 \times \text{side}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let side of the square (in cm) $= a$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
a \times a                                      &= 64 \\\\
a^{2}                                           &= 64 \\\\
a                                               &= \sqrt {64} \\\\
a                                               &= 8 \ \text{cm}
\end{aligned}
$

Side of the square $= 8 \ \text{cm}$

Let's find the perimeter of the square.

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Perimeter of the square}                  &= 4 \times 8 \\\\
                                                &= 32 \ \text{cm}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$32 \ \text{cm}$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

</code></pre>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

The plan on the right shows a garden. There is a $2 \ \text{m}$ wide path around the edge of the garden, with a swimming pool inside the path. Find the area of the path.

![papers/reigate-grammar-school/2019/sample-paper-6/s01-q29-i00.png](/assets/papers/reigate-grammar-school/2019/sample-paper-6/s01-q29-i00.png "papers/reigate-grammar-school/2019/sample-paper-6/s01-q29-i00.png")

</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">The plan on the right shows a garden. There is a $2 \ \text{m}$ wide path around the edge of the garden, with a swimming pool inside the path. Find the area of the path.
<emptyline>
%image%
</code></pre>

<div class="workings">

$

<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">If a square has an area of $36 \text{cm}^2$, what is its perimeter?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Area of square} = \text{side} \times \text{side}$

- ABBR: $\text{Perimeter of square} = 4 \times \text{side}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let the side of the square (in cm) $= a$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
a \times a                                      &= 36 \\\\
a^{2}                                           &= 36 \\\\
a                                               &= \sqrt {36} \\\\
a                                               &= 6 \ \text{cm}
\end{aligned}
$

Side of the square $= 6 \ \text{cm}$

Let's find the perimeter of the square.

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Perimeter of the square}                  &= 4 \times 6 \\\\
                                                &= 24 \ \text{cm}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$24 \ \text{cm}$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question_envelope rag_not_found subquestion">
<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

What is the side length of the square?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">What is the side length of the square?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Perimeter of square} = 4 \times \text{side}$

- ABBR: $\text{Area of square} = \text{side} \times \text{side}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let the side length of the square $= a$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
a \times a                  &= 4 \times a \\\\
a \times a                  &= 4a \\\\
a                           &= \dfrac {4 a} {a} \\\\
a                           &= \dfrac {4 \cancel{a}} {\cancel{a}} \\\\
a                           &= 4
\end{aligned}
$

Side length of the square $= 4$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$4$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question_envelope rag_not_found subquestion">
<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Now I want to draw a rectangle that is twice as long as it is wide. However, its perimeter is still equal in size to its area. What will the length of the rectangle be?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Now I want to draw a rectangle that is twice as long as it is wide. However, its perimeter is still equal in size to its area. What will the length of the rectangle be?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Area of rectangle} = \text{length} \times \text{width}$

- ABBR: $\text{Perimeter of rectangle} = 2 \times (\text{length} + \text{width})$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let the width of the rectangle $= a$

Length of the rectangle $= 2a$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
2a \times a                         &= 2 \times (2a + a) \\\\
2a \times a                         &= 2 \times 3a \\\\
a                                   &= \dfrac{2 \times 3 \times a} {2 \times a} \\\\
a                                   &= \dfrac{\cancel{2} \times 3 \times \cancel{a}} {\cancel{2} \times \cancel{a}} \\\\
a                                   &= 3
\end{aligned}
$

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Length of rectangle} &= 2 \times a \\\\
                            &= 2 \times 3 \\\\
                            &= 6 \ \text{cm}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$6 \ \text{cm}$

</div>
</div>

<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Tips Raw</strong>

</code></pre>

- rtq_abbr_formula_katex_expanded_perimeter_rectangle
</code></pre>
</div>

<div class="workingcodeblock_title">

</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Let the width of the rectangle $= a$
<emptyline>

<emptyline>

a                                   &= \dfrac{2 \times 3 \times a} {2 \times a} \\\\
a                                   &= \dfrac{\cancel{2} \times 3 \times \cancel{a}} {\cancel{2} \times \cancel{a}} \\\\
a                                   &= 3
\end{aligned}
$
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Length of rectangle} &= 2 \times a \\\\
                            &= 2 \times 3 \\\\
                            &= 6 \ \text{cm}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

The   length   of a  rectangle   is twice its   width.   Its   area  is **$98 \ \text{cm}^2$** .  Find  its   length   and   width.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">The   length   of a  rectangle   is twice its   width.   Its   area  is **$98 \ \text{cm}^2$** .  Find  its   length   and   width.
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Area of rectangle} = \text{length} \times \text{width}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let the width of the  rectangle (in cm) $= w$

Length $= 2 w$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}                            
2\times w \times w  &= 98 \\\\\
2 \times w^2        &= 98 \\\\
w^2                 &= \dfrac {98} {2} \\\\
w^2                 &= \dfrac {49 \times 2} {2} \\\\
w^2                 &= \dfrac {49 \times \cancel {2} } {\cancel {2} } \\\\
w^2                 &= 49 \\\\
w                   &= \sqrt {49} \\\\
w                   &= 7
\end{aligned}
$

Width $= 7 \ \text{cm}$

$
\begin{aligned}                            
\text {Length}    &= 2 \times w \\\\
                  &= 2 \times 7 \\\\
                  &= 14 \ \text{cm}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$14 \ \text{cm}, 7 \ \text{cm}$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Tips Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="formulacodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="formulacodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Formulas Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">- rtq_abbr_formula_katex_area_rectangle_expanded
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Let the width of the  rectangle (in cm) $= w$
<emptyline>
Length $= 2 w$
<emptyline>
As per the question,
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}                            
2\times w \times w  &= 98 \\\\\
2 \times w^2        &= 98 \\\\
w^2                 &= \dfrac {98} {2} \\\\
w^2                 &= \dfrac {49 \times 2} {2} \\\\
w^2                 &= \dfrac {49 \times \cancel {2} } {\cancel {2} } \\\\
w^2                 &= 49 \\\\
w                   &= \sqrt {49} \\\\
w                   &= 7
\end{aligned}
$
<emptyline>
Width $= 7 \ \text{cm}$
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}                            
\text {Length}    &= 2 \times w \\\\
                  &= 2 \times 7 \\\\
                  &= 14 \ \text{cm}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="answercodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Option Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%</code></pre>
</div>

</div>

<div class="answercodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Answer Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">$14 \ \text{cm}, 7 \ \text{cm}$
</code></pre>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Find the area and perimeter of this shape. Give the units with your answer.

![papers/st-francis-college/2017/sample-paper-1/s01-q26-i00.png](/assets/papers/st-francis-college/2017/sample-paper-1/s01-q26-i00.png "papers/st-francis-college/2017/sample-paper-1/s01-q26-i00.png")

<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Find the area and perimeter of this shape. Give the units with your answer.
<emptyline>

</code></pre>

<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Perimeter of rectangle} = 2 \times (\text{length} + \text{width})$

- ABBR: $\text{Area of rectangle} = \text{length} \times \text{width}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

As per the diagram,

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Area} &= 9 \times 7 \\\\
             &= 63 \ \text{m}^2
\end{aligned}
$

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Perimeter} &= 2 \times (9 + 7) \\\\
                  &= 2 \times 16 \\\\
                  &= 32 \ \text{m}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

Area $=$ $63 \ \text{m}^2$ 

</div>

**Answer**

Perimeter $=$ $32 \ \text{m}$

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Find the area of this shape. Give the units with your answer.

![papers/st-francis-college/2017/sample-paper-1/s01-q27-i00.png](/assets/papers/st-francis-college/2017/sample-paper-1/s01-q27-i00.png "papers/st-francis-college/2017/sample-paper-1/s01-q27-i00.png")

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Find the area of this shape. Give the units with your answer.
<emptyline>
%image%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Area of triangle} = \dfrac{1}{2} \times \text{base} \times \text{height}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

As per the figure,

Height $= 6 \text{cm}$

Base $= 10 \text{cm}$

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Area}             &= \dfrac {1} {2} \times 10 \times 6 \\\\
                         &= \dfrac {10 \times 3 \times 2} {2} \\\\
                         &= \dfrac {10 \times 3 \times \cancel{2}} {\cancel{2}} \\\\
                         &= 30 \ \text{cm}^2
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$30 \ \text{cm}^2$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Calculate the area of the trapezium.

![papers/streatham-and-clapham-high-school/2019/sample-paper-2/s01-q15-i00.png](/assets/papers/streatham-and-clapham-high-school/2019/sample-paper-2/s01-q15-i00.png "papers/streatham-and-clapham-high-school/2019/sample-paper-2/s01-q15-i00.png")

- $50 \ \text{cm}^{2}$
- $44 \ \text{cm}^{2}$
- $40 \ \text{cm}^{2}$
- $42 \ \text{cm}^{2}$
- $48 \ \text{cm}^{2}$ 

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Calculate the area of the trapezium.
<emptyline>
%image%
<emptyline>
- $50 \ \text{cm}^{2}$
- $44 \ \text{cm}^{2}$
- $40 \ \text{cm}^{2}$
- $42 \ \text{cm}^{2}$
- $48 \ \text{cm}^{2}$ 
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Area of trapezium} = \dfrac {\text{length} + \text{width}} {2} \times \text {height}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

As per the diagram,

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Area}                 &= \dfrac {8+12} {2}\times 5\\\\
                            &= \dfrac {20} {2}\times 5\\\\
                            &= \dfrac {2 \times 10} {2}\times 5\\\\
                            &= \dfrac { \cancel 2 \times 10} {\cancel 2}\times 5\\\\
                            &= 10 \times 5\\\\
                            &= 50 \ \text{cm}^{2}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="option" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Option**

<p>A</p>
</div>
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$50 \ \text{cm}^{2}$

</div>
</div>

Its length is $8 \ \text{m}$.

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Work out the width of Farmer Jack's field.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Work out the width of Farmer Jack's field.
</code></pre>

<div class="workings">

</div>

<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Area of rectangle} = \text{length} \times \text{width}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let the width of the field $= w$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
8 \times w                 &= 24 \\\\
w                          &= \dfrac {24} {8} \\\\
w                          &= \dfrac {3 \times 8} {8} \\\\
w                          &= \dfrac {3 \times \cancel{8}} {\cancel{8}} \\\\
w                          &= 3 \ \text{m}
\end{aligned}
$

Width of the rectangle field $= 3 \ \text{m}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$3 \ \text{m}$

</div>

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Work out the perimeter of Farmer Jack's field.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Work out the perimeter of Farmer Jack's field.
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Perimeter of rectangle} = 2 \times (\text{length} + \text{width})$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Perimeter}                &= 2 \times (8 + 3) \\\\
                                 &= 2 \times 11 \\\\
                                 &= 22 \ \text{m}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>

<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<li class="questionContainer">
<div class="question_envelope rag_not_found subquestion">
<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Farmer Giles' field has a perimeter of $20 \ \text{m}$.

The width of Farmer Giles' field is $4 \ \text{m}$. 

![papers/the-independent-schools-examinations-board/2016/sample-paper-5/s01-q20-s03-i00.png](/assets/papers/the-independent-schools-examinations-board/2016/sample-paper-5/s01-q20-s03-i00.png "papers/the-independent-schools-examinations-board/2016/sample-paper-5/s01-q20-s03-i00.png")

Work out the length of Farmer Giles' field.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Farmer Giles' field has a perimeter of $20 \ \text{m}$.
<emptyline>
The width of Farmer Giles' field is $4 \ \text{m}$. 
<emptyline>
%image%
<emptyline>
Work out the length of Farmer Giles' field.
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Perimeter of rectangle} = 2 \times (\text{length} + \text{width})$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let the length $= l$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
2 \times (4 + l)                 &= 20 \\\\
8 + 2l                           &= 20 \\\\
2l                               &= 20 - 8 \\\\
2l                               &= 12 \\\\
l                                &= \dfrac {12} {2} \\\\
l                                &= \dfrac {2 \times 6} {2} \\\\
l                                &= \dfrac {\cancel {2} \times 6} {\cancel {2}} \\\\
l                                &= 6 \ \text{m}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$6 \ \text{m}$

</div>
</div>

<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

</div>

</code></pre>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

This shape is made from $9$ identical squares.

![papers/the-london-independent-girls-schools-consortium/2008/group-2/s01-q31-i00.png](/assets/papers/the-london-independent-girls-schools-consortium/2008/group-2/s01-q31-i00.png "papers/the-london-independent-girls-schools-consortium/2008/group-2/s01-q31-i00.png")

The area of the shape is $144 \ \text{cm}^2$.

What is the perimeter of the shape?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">This shape is made from $9$ identical squares.
<emptyline>
%image%
<emptyline>
The area of the shape is $144 \ \text{cm}^2$.
<emptyline>
What is the perimeter of the shape?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Area of square} = \text{side} \times \text{side}$

- ABBR: $\text{Perimeter of square} = 4 \times \text{side}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let the side of the square $= s$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
9 \times s ^ 2  &= 144 \\\\
s ^ 2           &= \dfrac {144} {9} \\\\
s ^ 2           &= \dfrac {16 \times 9} {9} \\\\
s ^ 2           &= \dfrac {16 \times \cancel {9}} {\cancel {9}} \\\\
s ^ 2           &= 16 \\\\
s               &= \sqrt {16} \\\\
s               &= 4 \ \text{cm}
\end{aligned}
$

*TODO: Add a description on counting all the sides of the shape which makes the perimeter*.

Let's find the perimeter of the shape.

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Perimeter} &= 20 \times 4 \\\\
                 &= 80 \ \text{cm}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$80 \ \text{cm}$

</div>
</div>

<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

</div>

- rtq_abbr_formula_katex_expanded_perimeter_square
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<emptyline>

                 &= 80 \ \text{cm}

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

A square has a perimeter of $8\ \text{cm}$. Five of these squares are put together in a line to make a rectangle. 

What is the area of the rectangle?

![papers/the-london-independent-girls-schools-consortium/2009/group-2/s01-q18-i00.png](/assets/papers/the-london-independent-girls-schools-consortium/2009/group-2/s01-q18-i00.png "papers/the-london-independent-girls-schools-consortium/2009/group-2/s01-q18-i00.png")

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">A square has a perimeter of $8\ \text{cm}$. Five of these squares are put together in a line to make a rectangle. 
<emptyline>
What is the area of the rectangle?
<emptyline>
%image%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Perimeter of square} = 4 \times \text{side}$

- ABBR: $\text{Area of square} = \text{side} \times \text{side}$

- ABBR: $\text{Area of rectangle} = \text{length} \times \text{width}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let the side of the square $= s$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
4 \times s                 &= 8 \\\\
s                          &= \dfrac {8} {4} \\\\
s                          &= \dfrac {4 \times 2} {4} \\\\
s                          &= \dfrac {\cancel {4} \times 2} {\cancel {4}} \\\\
s                          &= 2 \ \text{cm}
\end{aligned}
$

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Area of one square} &= 2 \times 2 \\\\
                           &= 4 \ \text{cm}^2 \\\\
\text {Area of rectangle}  &= 5 \times \text{Area of one square} \\\\
                           &= 5 \times 4 \\\\
                           &= 20 \ \text{cm} ^ 2
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$20 \ \text{cm} ^ 2$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Tips Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="formulacodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="formulacodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Formulas Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">- rtq_abbr_formula_katex_expanded_perimeter_square
- rtq_abbr_formula_katex_area_square_expanded
- rtq_abbr_formula_katex_area_rectangle_expanded
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Let the side of the square $= s$
<emptyline>
As per the question,
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
4 \times s                 &= 8 \\\\
s                          &= \dfrac {8} {4} \\\\
s                          &= \dfrac {4 \times 2} {4} \\\\
s                          &= \dfrac {\cancel {4} \times 2} {\cancel {4}} \\\\
s                          &= 2 \ \text{cm}
\end{aligned}
$
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Area of one square} &= 2 \times 2 \\\\
                           &= 4 \ \text{cm}^2 \\\\
\text {Area of rectangle}  &= 5 \times \text{Area of one square} \\\\
                           &= 5 \times 4 \\\\
                           &= 20 \ \text{cm} ^ 2
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="answercodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Option Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="answercodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Key Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="answercodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Answer Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">$20 \ \text{cm} ^ 2$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
