<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

This shape has an area of $63 \ \text{cm}^2$.

It is made from square tiles.

Find the perimeter of the shape.

![papers/the-london-independent-girls-schools-consortium/2011/group-2/s01-q26-i00.png](/assets/papers/the-london-independent-girls-schools-consortium/2011/group-2/s01-q26-i00.png "papers/the-london-independent-girls-schools-consortium/2011/group-2/s01-q26-i00.png")

<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">This shape has an area of $63 \ \text{cm}^2$.
<emptyline>
It is made from square tiles.
<emptyline>
Find the perimeter of the shape.
<emptyline>
%image%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">

</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Area of square} = \text{side} \times \text{side}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let the side of square tile $= s$

As per the diagram,

$
\begin{aligned}
7 \times (s \times s)    &= 63 \\\\
7 \times s^2             &= 63 \\\\
s ^ 2                    &= \dfrac {63} {7} \\\\
s ^ 2                    &= \dfrac {7 \times 9} {7} \\\\
s ^ 2                    &= \dfrac {\cancel {7} \times 9} {\cancel {7}} \\\\
s ^ 2                    &= 9 \\\\
s                        &= \sqrt 9 \\\\
s                        &= 3 \ \text{cm}
\end{aligned}
$

*TODO: add a description to count all the sides which add upto perimeter*

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Perimeter of the shape} &= 16 \times s \\\\
                              &= 16 \times 3 \\\\
                              &= 48 \ \text{cm}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$48 \ \text{cm}$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

A photograph, which measures $12 \ \text{centimetres}$ by $15 \ \text{centimetres}$, is 
mounted on a piece of red card so that there is a border 
of $3 \ \text{centimetres}$ all the way round the photograph.

![papers/the-london-independent-girls-schools-consortium/2012/group-2/s01-q39-i00.png](/assets/papers/the-london-independent-girls-schools-consortium/2012/group-2/s01-q39-i00.png "papers/the-london-independent-girls-schools-consortium/2012/group-2/s01-q39-i00.png")

What area of red card is showing?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">A photograph, which measures $12 \ \text{centimetres}$ by $15 \ \text{centimetres}$, is 
mounted on a piece of red card so that there is a border 
of $3 \ \text{centimetres}$ all the way round the photograph.
<emptyline>
%image%
<emptyline>
What area of red card is showing?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Area of rectangle} = \text{length} \times \text{width}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Length of the card}      &= 15 + 2 \times 3 \\\\
                                &= 15 + 6 \\\\
                                &= 21 \ \text{cm} \\\\
\text {Width of the card}       &= 12 + 2 \times 3 \\\\
                                &= 12 + 6 \\\\
                                &= 18 \ \text{cm} \\\\
\text{Area of the red card}     &= \text{Total area} - \text{Area of photograph} \\\\
                                &= 21 \times 18 - 15 \times 12 \\\\
                                &= 378 - 180 \\\\
                                &= 198 \ \text{cm} ^ 2
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$198 \ \text{cm} ^ 2$

</div>
</div>

</div>

</code></pre>
</div>

</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">- rtq_abbr_formula_katex_area_rectangle_expanded
</code></pre>
</div>

                                &= 12 + 6 \\\\
                                &= 18 \ \text{cm} \\\\

                                &= 21 \times 18 - 15 \times 12 \\\\

\end{aligned}
$

<li class="questionContainer">
<div class="question_envelope rag_g0 rag_prns question">
<div class="uuid">
<p>C59005D5-EA4A-432B-99E9-DF224B680D47</p>
</div>
<div class="papername">
<p>the-london-independent-girls-schools-consortium--11-plus--maths--2015--group-2:1:32</p>
</div>
<div class="rag">
<p>Waffles Woof</p>

<div class="rag">

<div class="rag">

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

In the diagrams below, the perimeter of the square is equal to the perimeter of the isosceles triangle.

Work out the length of the side marked $d$.

![papers/the-london-independent-girls-schools-consortium/2015/group-2/s01-q32-i00.png](/assets/papers/the-london-independent-girls-schools-consortium/2015/group-2/s01-q32-i00.png "papers/the-london-independent-girls-schools-consortium/2015/group-2/s01-q32-i00.png")
![papers/the-london-independent-girls-schools-consortium/2015/group-2/s01-q32-i01.png](/assets/papers/the-london-independent-girls-schools-consortium/2015/group-2/s01-q32-i01.png "papers/the-london-independent-girls-schools-consortium/2015/group-2/s01-q32-i01.png")

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">In the diagrams below, the perimeter of the square is equal to the perimeter of the isosceles triangle.
<emptyline>
Work out the length of the side marked $d$.
<emptyline>
%image%
%image%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Perimeter of square} = 4 \times \text{side}$

- ABBR: $\text{Perimeter of triangle} = \text{Sum of all sides}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Perimeter of the square}     &= 4 \times \text {Sides} \\\\
                                    &= 4 \times 9 \\\\
                                    &= 36 \ \text{cm}
\end{aligned}
$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Perimeter of the triangle}   &= 36 \\\\
d + d + 20                          &= 36 \\\\
2d + 20                             &= 36 \\\\
2d                                  &= 36 - 20 \\\\
2d                                  &= 16 \\\\
d                                   &= \dfrac {16} {2} \\\\
d                                   &= \dfrac {8 \times 2} {2} \\\\
d                                   &= \dfrac {8 \times \cancel {2}} {\cancel {2}} \\\\
d                                   &= 8 \ \text{cm}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$8 \ \text{cm}$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Tips Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="formulacodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="formulacodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Formulas Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">- rtq_abbr_formula_katex_expanded_perimeter_square
- rtq_abbr_formula_katex_expanded_perimeter_triangle
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Perimeter of the square}     &= 4 \times \text {Sides} \\\\
                                    &= 4 \times 9 \\\\
                                    &= 36 \ \text{cm}
\end{aligned}
$
<emptyline>
As per the question,
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Perimeter of the triangle}   &= 36 \\\\
d + d + 20                          &= 36 \\\\
2d + 20                             &= 36 \\\\
2d                                  &= 36 - 20 \\\\
2d                                  &= 16 \\\\
d                                   &= \dfrac {16} {2} \\\\
d                                   &= \dfrac {8 \times 2} {2} \\\\
d                                   &= \dfrac {8 \times \cancel {2}} {\cancel {2}} \\\\
d                                   &= 8 \ \text{cm}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

$1 \ \text{foot}$ is equal to $12 \ \text{inches}$. 
Paul tiles a wall which is $10 \ \text{feet}$ long 
and $6 \ \text{feet}$ high. 
He uses square tiles which are $8 \ \text{inches}$ long on 
each side. How many tiles does he need to tile the wall?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">$1 \ \text{foot}$ is equal to $12 \ \text{inches}$. 
Paul tiles a wall which is $10 \ \text{feet}$ long 
and $6 \ \text{feet}$ high. 
He uses square tiles which are $8 \ \text{inches}$ long on 
each side. How many tiles does he need to tile the wall?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
- ABBR: It is really important to use the right units in calculations and convert where required.
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Area of rectangle} = \text{length} \times \text{width}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

The wall measurement is given in feet while the tile measurement is given in inches.

Let's convert the wall measurements into inches.

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Number of tiles}  &= \dfrac {\text{Area of the Wall}} {\text{Area of a Tile}} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {10 \ \text{feet} \times 6 \ \text{feet} } { 8 \ \text{inches} \times 8 \ \text{inches} } \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {10 \times 12 \ \text{inches} \times 6 \times 12 \ \text{inches} } { 8 \ \text{inches} \times 8 \ \text{inches} } \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {10 \times 12 \times 6 \times 12 } { 8 \times 8 } \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {2 \times 5 \times 4 \times 3 \times 2 \times 3 \times 4 \times 3 } { 4 \times 2 \times 4 \times 2 } \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {\cancel{2} \times 5 \times \cancel{4} \times 3 \times \cancel{2} \times 3 \times \cancel{4} \times 3 } { \cancel{4} \times \cancel{2} \times \cancel{4} \times \cancel{2} } \\\\
                        &= 5 \times 3 \times 3 \times 3 \\\\
                        &= 135
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$135$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Tips Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">- rtq_abbr_note_md_unit
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="formulacodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="formulacodeblock_title">

</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">- rtq_abbr_formula_katex_area_rectangle_expanded
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">The wall measurement is given in feet while the tile measurement is given in inches.
<emptyline>
Let's convert the wall measurements into inches.
<emptyline>
As per the question,
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}

                        &= \dfrac {10 \ \text{feet} \times 6 \ \text{feet} } { 8 \ \text{inches} \times 8 \ \text{inches} } \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {10 \times 12 \ \text{inches} \times 6 \times 12 \ \text{inches} } { 8 \ \text{inches} \times 8 \ \text{inches} } \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {10 \times 12 \times 6 \times 12 } { 8 \times 8 } \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {2 \times 5 \times 4 \times 3 \times 2 \times 3 \times 4 \times 3 } { 4 \times 2 \times 4 \times 2 } \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {\cancel{2} \times 5 \times \cancel{4} \times 3 \times \cancel{2} \times 3 \times \cancel{4} \times 3 } { \cancel{4} \times \cancel{2} \times \cancel{4} \times \cancel{2} } \\\\
                        &= 5 \times 3 \times 3 \times 3 \\\\

$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Daniel has eight cubes, each of side $1 \ \text{cm}$. He glues them 
together to make the letter $F$, which he then 
paints. What area is covered with paint?

![papers/the-manchester-grammar-school/2010/arithmetic-1/s01-q19-i00.png](/assets/papers/the-manchester-grammar-school/2010/arithmetic-1/s01-q19-i00.png "papers/the-manchester-grammar-school/2010/arithmetic-1/s01-q19-i00.png")

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Daniel has eight cubes, each of side $1 \ \text{cm}$. He glues them 
together to make the letter $F$, which he then 
paints. What area is covered with paint?
<emptyline>
%image%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Area of square} = \text{side} \times \text{side}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

As per the diagram, 

*TODO: add the explanation to count the number of faces in the diagram.*

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Area of 1 face}      &=  1 \times 1 \ \text{cm}^2  \\\\
\text {Area of 34 faces}    &=  34 \times 1 \\\\
                            &=  34 \ \text{cm}^2
\end{aligned}
$

Area covered with paint $= 34 \ \text{cm}^2$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$34 \ \text{cm}^2$

</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

The area of a rectangle is $72$ $ \ \text{cm} ^2$. The length of the rectangle is $1 \ \text{cm}$ more than the width.

Work out the length of the perimeter of the rectangle.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">The area of a rectangle is $72$ $ \ \text{cm} ^2$. The length of the rectangle is $1 \ \text{cm}$ more than the width.
<emptyline>
Work out the length of the perimeter of the rectangle.
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Area of rectangle} = \text{length} \times \text{width}$

- ABBR: $\text{Perimeter of rectangle} = 2 \times (\text{length} + \text{width})$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let the width of the rectangle in ($\text{cm}$) $= a$

Length of the rectangle in ($\text{cm}$) $= a +1$

As per the question, 

$a \times (a + 1) = 72$

ABBR: 

*Note*: The two equations form a quadratic equation.

*Note*: This is a quadratic equation.

Solving quadratic equations is beyond the scope and any such equations can be easily inferred by trial and error method.


$
\begin{aligned}
a \times (a + 1)    &= 72 \\\\
8 \times (8 + 1)    &= 72 \\\\
8 \times 9          &= 72 \\\\
a                   &= 8 \ \text{cm} \\\\
\text {Width}       &= 8 \ \text{cm} \\\\
\text {Length}      &= a + 1 \\\\
                    &= 9 + 1 \\\\
                    &= 10 \ \text{cm}
\end{aligned}
$

Let's find the perimeter of the rectangle.

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Perimeter}    &= 2 \times (8 + 9) \\\\
                    &= 2 \times 17 \\\\
                    &= 34 \ \text{cm}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$34 \ \text{cm}$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

</div>

</code></pre>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Two rectangles have the same area. One rectangle is $28 \ \text{cm}$ long and $12 \ \text{cm}$ wide. The second rectangle has width $7 \ \text{cm}$. What is the length of this rectangle?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Two rectangles have the same area. One rectangle is $28 \ \text{cm}$ long and $12 \ \text{cm}$ wide. The second rectangle has width $7 \ \text{cm}$. What is the length of this rectangle?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Area of rectangle} = \text{length} \times \text{width}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let the length of the second rectangle $= l$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
l \times 7  &= 28 \times 12 \\\\
l           &= \dfrac {28 \times 12} { 7} \\\\
l           &= \dfrac {7 \times 4 \times 12} { 7} \\\\
l           &= \dfrac {\cancel{7} \times 4 \times 12} { \cancel{7}} \\\\
l           &= 4 \times 12 \\\\
l           &= 48 \ \text{cm}
\end{aligned}  
$

Length of the second rectangle $= 48 \ \text{cm}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$48 \ \text{cm}$

</div>
</div>

<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

</code></pre>
</div>

<div class="formulacodeblock_title">

</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">- rtq_abbr_formula_katex_area_rectangle_expanded
</code></pre>
</div>

<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Let the length of the second rectangle $= l$
<emptyline>
As per the question,
<emptyline>
$

l           &= \dfrac {28 \times 12} { 7} \\\\

l           &= 4 \times 12 \\\\
l           &= 48 \ \text{cm}
\end{aligned}  
$

</code></pre>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

A rectangle is $4 \ \text{cm}$ longer than it is wide. The 
distance all the way round the rectangle (its perimeter) 
is $32 \ \text{cm}$.  What is the area of the rectangle?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">A rectangle is $4 \ \text{cm}$ longer than it is wide. The 
distance all the way round the rectangle (its perimeter) 
is $32 \ \text{cm}$.  What is the area of the rectangle?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Area of rectangle} = \text{length} \times \text{width}$

- ABBR: $\text{Perimeter of rectangle} = 2 \times (\text{length} + \text{width})$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let the width of the rectangle (in $\text{cm}$) $= a$

Length of the rectangle $= a + 4$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
2 \times (a + (a + 4))    &= 32 \\\\
2 \times (2a + 4)         &= 32 \\\\
4a + 8                    &= 32 \\\\
4a                        &= 32 - 8 \\\\
4a                        &= 24 \\\\
a                         &= \dfrac {24} {4} \\\\
a                         &= \dfrac {4 \times 6} {4} \\\\
a                         &= \dfrac {\cancel {4} \times 6} {\cancel {4}} \\\\
a                         &= 6 \ \text{cm} \\\\
\text{Width}              &= 6 \ \text{cm} \\\\
\text{Length}             &= a + 4 \\\\
                          &= 6 + 4 \\\\
                          &= 10 \ \text{cm} \\\\
\text{Area}               &= 10 \times 6 \\\\
                          &= 60 \ \text{cm}^{2}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$60 \ \text{cm}^{2}$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Tips Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>

<div class="formulacodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Formulas Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">- rtq_abbr_formula_katex_area_rectangle_expanded
- rtq_abbr_formula_katex_expanded_perimeter_rectangle
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Let the width of the rectangle (in $\text{cm}$) $= a$
<emptyline>
Length of the rectangle $= a + 4$
<emptyline>
As per the question,
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
2 \times (a + (a + 4))    &= 32 \\\\
2 \times (2a + 4)         &= 32 \\\\
4a + 8                    &= 32 \\\\
4a                        &= 32 - 8 \\\\
4a                        &= 24 \\\\
a                         &= \dfrac {24} {4} \\\\
a                         &= \dfrac {4 \times 6} {4} \\\\
a                         &= \dfrac {\cancel {4} \times 6} {\cancel {4}} \\\\
a                         &= 6 \ \text{cm} \\\\
\text{Width}              &= 6 \ \text{cm} \\\\
\text{Length}             &= a + 4 \\\\
                          &= 6 + 4 \\\\
                          &= 10 \ \text{cm} \\\\
\text{Area}               &= 10 \times 6 \\\\
                          &= 60 \ \text{cm}^{2}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="answercodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Option Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="answercodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Key Raw</strong>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

In the following diagram the two shaded rectangles have equal areas. What is the **total** shaded area?

![papers/the-manchester-grammar-school/2017/arithmetic-a/s01-q15-i00.png](/assets/papers/the-manchester-grammar-school/2017/arithmetic-a/s01-q15-i00.png "papers/the-manchester-grammar-school/2017/arithmetic-a/s01-q15-i00.png")

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">In the following diagram the two shaded rectangles have equal areas. What is the **total** shaded area?
<emptyline>
%image%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Area of rectangle} = \text{length} \times \text{width}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let the length of the shaded left bottom rectangle (in $\text{cm}$) $= a$

Length of the shaded right top rectangle (in $\text{cm}$) $= 14 - a$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
3 \times a  &= 4 \times (14 - a) \\\\
3a          &= 56 - 4a \\\\
3a + 4a     &= 56 \\\\
7a          &= 56 \\\\
a           &= \dfrac {56} {7} \\\\
a           &= \dfrac {7 \times 8} {7} \\\\
a           &= \dfrac {\cancel {7} \times 8} {\cancel{7}} \\\\
a           &= 8 \ \text{cm}
\end{aligned}
$

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Total shaded area}    &= 2 \times (3 \times 8) \\\\
                            &= 2 \times 24 \\\\
                            &= 48 \ \text{cm}^2
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$48 \ \text{cm}^2$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">- rtq_abbr_formula_katex_area_rectangle_expanded
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>

<emptyline>

\end{aligned}
$
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Total shaded area}    &= 2 \times (3 \times 8) \\\\
                            &= 2 \times 24 \\\\
                            &= 48 \ \text{cm}^2
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

St Hugh's School hall is in the shape of a large box or cuboid.  
The area of the floor is $200 \ \text{m}^2$, the area of one of the 
side walls is $150 \ \text{m}^2$ and the area of an end wall is $48 \ \text{m}^2$. 

What is the height, in metres, of the hall?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">St Hugh's School hall is in the shape of a large box or cuboid.  
The area of the floor is $200 \ \text{m}^2$, the area of one of the 
side walls is $150 \ \text{m}^2$ and the area of an end wall is $48 \ \text{m}^2$. 
<emptyline>
What is the height, in metres, of the hall?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Area} = \text{length} \times \text{width}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let the length of the school hall (in m) $= l$

Let the width of the school hall (in m) $= w$

Let the height of the school hall (in m) $= h$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
l \times h                              &= 150 \\\\
l                                       &= \dfrac {150} {h} \\\\
w \times h                              &= 48 \\\\
w                                       &= \dfrac{48}{h} \\\\
l \times w                              &= 200 \\\\
\dfrac {150} {h} \times \dfrac {48} {h} &= 200 \\\\
\dfrac {150 \times 48} {h^2}            &= 200 \\\\
\dfrac {150 \times 48} {200}            &= h^2 \\\\
h^2                                     &= \dfrac {150 \times 48} {200} \\\\
h^2                                     &= \dfrac {50 \times 3 \times 12 \times 4} {50 \times 4} \\\\
h^2                                     &= \dfrac {\cancel{50} \times 3 \times 12 \times \cancel{4}} {\cancel{50} \times \cancel{4}} \\\\
h^2                                     &= 3 \times 12 \\\\
h^2                                     &= 36 \\\\
h                                       &= \sqrt {36} \\\\
h                                       &= 6 \ \text{m}
\end{aligned}
$

Height of the school hall (in m) $= 6  \ \text{m}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$6 \ \text{m}$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Tips Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="formulacodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="formulacodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Formulas Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">- rtq_abbr_formula_katex_area_rectangle
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Let the length of the school hall (in m) $= l$
<emptyline>

<emptyline>

\dfrac {150 \times 48} {200}            &= h^2 \\\\
h^2                                     &= \dfrac {150 \times 48} {200} \\\\
h^2                                     &= \dfrac {50 \times 3 \times 12 \times 4} {50 \times 4} \\\\
h^2                                     &= \dfrac {\cancel{50} \times 3 \times 12 \times \cancel{4}} {\cancel{50} \times \cancel{4}} \\\\
h^2                                     &= 3 \times 12 \\\\
h^2                                     &= 36 \\\\
h                                       &= \sqrt {36} \\\\
h                                       &= 6 \ \text{m}
\end{aligned}
$
<emptyline>
Height of the school hall (in m) $= 6  \ \text{m}$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

In the picture below is the net of a cube. What would be the total surface area of the outside of the cube when the net is made into the cube?

![papers/the-manchester-grammar-school/2019/arithmetic-a/s01-q15-i00.png](/assets/papers/the-manchester-grammar-school/2019/arithmetic-a/s01-q15-i00.png "papers/the-manchester-grammar-school/2019/arithmetic-a/s01-q15-i00.png")

</div>

<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">In the picture below is the net of a cube. What would be the total surface area of the outside of the cube when the net is made into the cube?
<emptyline>
%image%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Surface area of cube} = 6 \times \text{edge} \times \text{edge}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let the side of the cube (in $\text{cm}$) $= a$

As per the diagram,

$
\begin{aligned}
3a      &= 15 \\\\
a       &= \dfrac {15} {3} \\\\
a       &= 5 \ \text{cm}
\end{aligned}
$

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Surface area}  &= 6 \times a \times a \\\\
                     &= 6 \times 5 \times 5 \\\\
                     &= 150 \ \text{cm}^{2}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$150 \ \text{cm}^2$

</div>

\text {Side of square in the middle}            &= \text {length of rectangle} - \text {width of rectangle} \\\\
                                                &= 7 - 3  \\\\
                                                &= 4 \ \text{cm} \\\\
\text {Perimeter of the square in the middle}   &= 4 \times 4 \\\\
                                                &= 16 \ \text{cm}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>

<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

$16 \ \text{cm}$

**Question**

The dimensions of a rectangle and triangle are given. The diagrams are not drawn accurately.

![papers/the-queens-school/sample/sample-paper-1/s01-q15-i00.png](/assets/papers/the-queens-school/sample/sample-paper-1/s01-q15-i00.png "papers/the-queens-school/sample/sample-paper-1/s01-q15-i00.png")     ![papers/the-queens-school/sample/sample-paper-1/s01-q15-i01.png](/assets/papers/the-queens-school/sample/sample-paper-1/s01-q15-i01.png "papers/the-queens-school/sample/sample-paper-1/s01-q15-i01.png")

They are joined together to make a new shape. 

![papers/the-queens-school/sample/sample-paper-1/s01-q15-i02.png](/assets/papers/the-queens-school/sample/sample-paper-1/s01-q15-i02.png "papers/the-queens-school/sample/sample-paper-1/s01-q15-i02.png")

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

![papers/the-queens-school/sample/sample-paper-1/s01-q24-i00.png](/assets/papers/the-queens-school/sample/sample-paper-1/s01-q24-i00.png "papers/the-queens-school/sample/sample-paper-1/s01-q24-i00.png")

A path of width $1.5\, \text{m}$ is built around the outside of a pond. The dimensions of the path are shown.

What is the area of the pond?

Make sure you state the units.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%image%
<emptyline>
A path of width $1.5\, \text{m}$ is built around the outside of a pond. The dimensions of the path are shown.
<emptyline>
What is the area of the pond?
<emptyline>
Make sure you state the units.
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

</div>

<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Area of rectangle} = \text{length} \times \text{width}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

Also show the measurement of border.*

*ABBR: TODO:: Add drawing ends.*


As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Length of the pond}  &= 11 - 2 \times 1.5 \\\\
                            &= 11 - 3 \\\\
                            &= 8 \ \text{m} \\\\
\text {Width of the pond}   &= 8 - 2 \times 1.5 \\\\
                            &= 8 - 3 \\\\
                            &= 5 \ \text{m}
\end{aligned}
$

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Area of the pond}     &= 8 \times 5 \\\\
                            &= 40 \ \text{cm}^2
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$40 \ \text{cm}^2$

</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Find the total area of the shaded regions.

*The diagram is not drawn accurately*.

![papers/tonbridge-school/sample/sample-paper-1/s01-q17-i00.png](/assets/papers/tonbridge-school/sample/sample-paper-1/s01-q17-i00.png "papers/tonbridge-school/sample/sample-paper-1/s01-q17-i00.png")

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Find the total area of the shaded regions.
<emptyline>
*The diagram is not drawn accurately*.
<emptyline>
%image%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Area of triangle} = \dfrac{1}{2} \times \text{base} \times \text{height}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

As per the diagram,

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Area of shaded regions}       &= \text{Area of left triangle} + \text{Area of right triangle} \\\\
                                    &= \dfrac {1} {2} \times 8.3 \times 3.784 + \dfrac {1} {2} \times 8.3 \times 2.216 \\\\
                                    &= \dfrac{8.3 \times 3.784} {2} + \dfrac {8.3 \times 2.216} {2} \\\\
                                    &= \dfrac{31.4072} {2} + \dfrac {18.3928} {2} \\\\
                                    &= \dfrac{31.4072 + 18.3928} {2} \\\\
                                    &= \dfrac{49.8} {2} \\\\
                                    &= \dfrac{2 \times 24.9} {2} \\\\
                                    &= \dfrac{\cancel {2} \times 24.9} {\cancel {2}} \\\\
                                    &= 24.9 \ \text{cm}^2
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$24.9 \ \text{cm}^2$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

How many small triangles would fit exactly into the larger rectangle?

![papers/warwick-school/sample/sample-paper-1/s01-q08-i00.png](/assets/papers/warwick-school/sample/sample-paper-1/s01-q08-i00.png "papers/warwick-school/sample/sample-paper-1/s01-q08-i00.png")

![papers/warwick-school/sample/sample-paper-1/s01-q08-i01.png](/assets/papers/warwick-school/sample/sample-paper-1/s01-q08-i01.png "papers/warwick-school/sample/sample-paper-1/s01-q08-i01.png")

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">How many small triangles would fit exactly into the larger rectangle?
<emptyline>
%image%
<emptyline>
%image%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
- ABBR: It is really important to use the right units in calculations and convert where required.
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Area of rectangle} = \text{length} \times \text{width}$

- ABBR: $\text{Area of triangle} = \dfrac{1}{2} \times \text{base} \times \text{height}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

The rectangle measurement is given in metres while the triangle measurement is given in cm.

Let's convert the rectangle measurements into $\text{cm}$.

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Number of tiles}  &= \dfrac {\text{Area of the rectangle}} {\text{Area of a triangle}} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {0.32 \ \text{m} \times 0.42 \ \text{m} } { \dfrac{1} {2} \times 8 \ \text{cm} \times 6 \ \text{cm} } \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {0.32 \times 100  \ \text{cm} \times 0.42 \times 100 \ \text{cm} } { \dfrac{1} {2} \times 8 \ \text{cm} \times 6 \ \text{cm} } \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {32 \times 42 \times 2} {8 \times 6} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {4 \times 8 \times 6 \times 7 \times 2 } {8 \times 6}  \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {4 \times \cancel{8} \times \cancel{6} \times 7 \times 2 } {\cancel{8} \times \cancel{6}}  \\\\
                        &= 4 \times 7 \times 2  \\\\
                        &= 56
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$56$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Tips Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">- rtq_abbr_note_md_unit
</code></pre>
</div>

</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">- rtq_abbr_formula_katex_area_rectangle_expanded
- rtq_abbr_formula_katex_area_triangle_expanded
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>

                        &= \dfrac {0.32 \times 100  \ \text{cm} \times 0.42 \times 100 \ \text{cm} } { \dfrac{1} {2} \times 8 \ \text{cm} \times 6 \ \text{cm} } \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {32 \times 42 \times 2} {8 \times 6} \\\\

                        &= 4 \times 7 \times 2  \\\\
                        &= 56

$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

The rectangle and triangle below have the same height.

![papers/warwick-school/sample/sample-paper-1/s01-q16-i00.png](/assets/papers/warwick-school/sample/sample-paper-1/s01-q16-i00.png "papers/warwick-school/sample/sample-paper-1/s01-q16-i00.png")

If the perimeter of the rectangle (all $4$ sides) is $32\ \text{m}$, what is the area of the triangle?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">The rectangle and triangle below have the same height.
<emptyline>
%image%
<emptyline>
If the perimeter of the rectangle (all $4$ sides) is $32\ \text{m}$, what is the area of the triangle?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Area of triangle} = \dfrac{1}{2} \times \text{base} \times \text{height}$

- ABBR: $\text{Perimeter of rectangle} = 2 \times (\text{length} + \text{width})$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let the width of rectangle $= w \ \text{m}$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
2 \times (7 + w)        &= 32 \\\\
14 + 2w                 &= 32 \\\\
2w                      &= 32 - 14 \\\\
2w                      &= 18 \\\\
w                       &= \dfrac{18} {2} \\\\
w                       &= \dfrac{2 \times 9} {2} \\\\
w                       &= \dfrac{\cancel{2} \times 9} {\cancel{2}} \\\\
w                       &= 9 \ \text{m}
\end{aligned}
$

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Base of the triangle}    &= 17 - 9 \\\\
                                &= 8 \ \text{m}
\end{aligned}
$

Let's find the area of the triangle.

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Area}             &= \dfrac{1} {2} \times 8 \times 7 \\\\
                        &= \dfrac{8 \times 7} {2} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac{2 \times 4 \times 7} {2} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac{\cancel {2} \times 4 \times 7} {\cancel {2}} \\\\
                        &= 4 \times 7 \\\\
                        &= 28 \ \text{m}^2
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$28 \ \text{m}^2$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Tips Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="formulacodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="formulacodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Formulas Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">- rtq_abbr_formula_katex_area_triangle_expanded
- rtq_abbr_formula_katex_expanded_perimeter_rectangle
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>

\end{aligned}
$
<emptyline>

                                &= 8 \ \text{m}

$
<emptyline>

<emptyline>

                        &= \dfrac{2 \times 4 \times 7} {2} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac{\cancel {2} \times 4 \times 7} {\cancel {2}} \\\\

</div>
<div class="answers">

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

The diagram below represents a rectangular grass lawn surrounded by a gravel path on the outside. 
The width of the path is $0.5\ \text{m}$. Gravel costs $\pounds 5.70\, \text{per}\, \text{m}^{2}$.

What is the cost of gravel required to cover the path?

![papers/warwick-school/sample/sample-paper-1/s01-q24-i00.png](/assets/papers/warwick-school/sample/sample-paper-1/s01-q24-i00.png "papers/warwick-school/sample/sample-paper-1/s01-q24-i00.png")

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">The diagram below represents a rectangular grass lawn surrounded by a gravel path on the outside. 
The width of the path is $0.5\ \text{m}$. Gravel costs $\pounds 5.70\, \text{per}\, \text{m}^{2}$.
<emptyline>
What is the cost of gravel required to cover the path?
<emptyline>
%image%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Area of rectangle} = \text{length} \times \text{width}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

*ABBR: TODO:: Add drawing starts.*

{/* ![missing image](/papers/missing_image.svg) */}
TODOIMAGE


*TODO:: Add the diagram with inner rectangle's length $5 \ \text{m}$ and width $7 \ \text{m}$ shown.*

*ABBR: TODO:: Add drawing ends.*


As per the diagram,

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Area of gravel path}  &= \text{Total Area} - \text{Area of grass lawn} \\\\
                            &= 8 \times 6 -  5 \times 7 \\\\
                            &= 48 - 35 \\\\
                            &= 13 \ \text{m}^2 \\\\
\text{Cost of gravel}       &= \text{Cost per } m^2 \times \text{Area of gravel path} \\\\
                            &= \pounds 5.70 \times 13 \\\\
                            &= \pounds 74.1
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$\pounds 74.1$

</div>
</div>
